### [1] [Phase-space dynamics and multicomponent wave-packet structures in the Dunkl anharmonic oscillator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00280)
*D. Ojeda-Guillén,R. D. Mota*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Dunkl各向异性谐振子（Kerr介质）的相空间动力学与多组分波包结构，通过解析推导Dunkl-Husimi Q函数及归一化角Husimi分布，揭示Dunkl参数μ对相干态激发权重与奇偶依赖相位累积的调控作用，导致角向各向异性与非对称相空间分离；在μ=0.5时出现不对称多峰Husimi图与早期重构（t=π/λ），在μ=1.0时保持多模性但分布展宽，并在基本非线性复兴时间实现波包重构；μ→0极限下恢复标准Kerr动力学。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Dunkl算符引入的广义对称性如何影响非线性量子系统（如Kerr介质）的相空间演化与波包重构特性，拓展对广义谐振子中奇偶耦合与非对称动力学的认知。

Method: 基于含时薛定谔方程求解Dunkl各向异性谐振子的时间演化态，严格推导Dunkl-Husimi Q函数与归一化角Husimi分布的解析表达式，结合参数扫描（μ=0.5, 1.0）分析相空间拓扑、峰高不对称性、分布展宽及非线性复兴行为。

Result: Dunkl参数μ调控相干态权重与奇偶相位差，导致角向各向异性与相空间非对称分离；μ=0.5时产生不对称多峰Husimi结构并提前于t=π/λ发生波包重构；μ=1.0时Husimi分布展宽但仍保持多模性与幺正演化；在基本非线性复兴时间实现完全波包重构；μ→0极限下精确退化为标准Kerr动力学。

Conclusion: Dunkl变形显著丰富了Kerr系统的相空间动力学景观，其参数μ不仅控制奇偶子空间间的干涉效应，还决定波包分裂、重构时序与几何对称性破缺程度，为操控量子非经典态与设计新型非线性量子器件提供理论依据。

Abstract: We study the phase-space dynamics and multicomponent wave-packet structures of the Dunkl anharmonic oscillator (Kerr medium). Starting from the time-evolved state, we derive analytical expressions for the Dunkl-Husimi $Q$-function and a normalized angular Husimi profile. The Dunkl parameter modifies the coherent-state excitation weights and the parity-dependent phase accumulation, producing angular anisotropy and asymmetric phase-space separation. For the half-integer deformation $\mu=0.5$, the dynamics generate asymmetric multicomponent Husimi patterns with unequal peak heights and an early reconstruction at $t=\pi/\lambda$. For the deformation $\mu=1.0$, the Husimi distribution exhibits broadening while retaining its multimodal structure and unitary evolution. At the fundamental nonlinear revival time, the wave packet is reconstructed for the selected parameter ratio. The standard Kerr dynamics are recovered in the limit $\mu\rightarrow 0$.

</details>


### [2] [Painlevé VI superintegrability and Heun-Racah algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00322)
*Ian Marquette,Sarah Post*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了欧氏平面上两个可分离于极坐标下的四阶量子超可积系统，其角向势由四参数第六类Painlevé超越函数决定；分别具有谐振子型与扩展库仑型径向项；给出了显式的四阶守恒量、三次对称代数及其Casimir算子，并构造了形变谐振子实现；在固定能量下，二者代数均为Heun–Racah代数的特例；角向结构函数相同（由Painlevé VI参数决定），径向结构函数则区分两类动力学；通过Levi-Civita变换与耦合常数变态建立了二者间参数、代数与表示的直接对应；结果覆盖完整四参数Painlevé VI族，推广了此前仅适用于有理参数的例外Jacobi模型；同时对比了相应经典Poisson代数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建并统一刻画由完整四参数Painlevé VI函数驱动的量子超可积系统，拓展已知的例外正交多项式模型至一般超越参数情形，并揭示不同径向动力学（谐振子vs库仑）在对称代数层面的共性与差异。

Method: 利用极坐标分离变量法构造哈密顿量；通过直接算符计算导出四阶量子守恒量；利用交换子代数推导三次对称代数及其中心元素（Casimir）；构造deformed-oscillator代数实现；运用Levi-Civita变换与耦合常数变态建立两类系统的严格映射；分析结构函数的角向/径向分解；对比经典Poisson代数结构。

Result: 1) 显式给出两个四阶量子超可积系统的四阶守恒量；2) 导出各自的三次对称代数及其Casimir；3) 构造deformed-oscillator代数实现；4) 证明二者在固定能量下均属Heun–Racah代数特例，共享同一角向结构因子（Painlevé VI参数决定），但径向因子不同；5) 建立Levi-Civita与CCM双重映射，实现Painlevé参数、代数、Casimir及表示的一一对应；6) 推广至全四参数Painlevé VI族，涵盖此前例外Jacobi情形为特例；7) 给出对应经典Poisson代数比较。

Conclusion: Painlevé VI超越函数作为角向势源，可系统生成两类本质不同的四阶量子超可积结构，其对称性由Heun–Racah代数统一描述；角向自由度完全编码Painlevé参数，而径向自由度决定动力学类型；Levi-Civita变换与耦合常数变态构成连接二者的对偶性框架；该工作将超可积理论从特殊正交多项式情形提升至一般非线性超越函数范畴。

Abstract: We study the two fourth-order quantum superintegrable systems in the Euclidean plane that separate in polar coordinates and whose angular potentials are determined by the general four-parameter sixth Painlevé transcendent. One system has an oscillator radial term and the other an extended-Coulomb radial term. For both systems we give the fourth-order quantum integral explicitly, derive the cubic symmetry algebra and its Casimir, and construct a deformed-oscillator realization. At fixed energy the two algebras are specializations of the Heun--Racah algebra. Their structure functions contain the same quartic angular factor, determined by the Painlevé~VI parameters, while different radial factors distinguish oscillator and Coulomb dynamics. The Coulomb integral is obtained by transforming the Cartesian Hermitian symmetry at the operator level. The lower-order terms generated by the polar coordinate change are essential for commutation with the Hamiltonian. The two quantum systems are also related by the Levi-Civita transformation and coupling-constant metamorphosis, which gives a direct correspondence between their Painlevé parameters, algebras, Casimirs and representations. Earlier exceptional-Jacobi models arise at special rational parameter values, whereas the results here apply to the full four-parameter Painlevé~VI family. We also compare the quantum results with the corresponding classical Poisson algebras.

</details>


### [3] [Stability of Solitons in the Parametrically Driven and Damped Nonlinear Schrödinger Equation in Arbitrary Dimension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00346)
*Fernando Carreño-Navas*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了参数驱动与阻尼非线性薛定谔（PDDNLS）方程在N维中幂律非线性下的孤子解谱稳定性，识别出两类稳态解Ψ±，通过线性化和角动量分解证明Ψ−恒不稳定，而Ψ+的不稳定性仅可能出现在l=0,1扇区，并给出基于Vakhitov–Kolokolov型判据与原点附近本征值分裂方向分析的解析不稳定性判据，同时揭示κ在小/大极限下的新现象。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解参数驱动与耗散系统中孤子解的谱稳定性，拓展一维结果至高维并揭示非线性指数κ与空间维数N耦合的新物理行为。

Method: 对PDDNLS方程中Ψ±解进行线性扰动分析，引入ε参数化将稳定性问题约化为两个无耗散薛定谔型算子L₀、L₁的本征值问题；采用球谐展开将谱问题按角动量扇区Sₗ分解；结合Vakhitov–Kolokolov单调性条件与原点邻域的局部本征值摄动分析，导出各扇区的解析不稳定性判据。

Result: Ψ₋在所有参数下恒谱不稳定；Ψ₊的不稳定性仅可能出现在l=0与l=1扇区，l≥2扇区无不稳定模，S₁上不产生复四重根；获得适用于任意N与κ的解析不稳定性阈值；发现κ→0⁺与κ→∞时出现一维情形未见的新失稳机制；给出N=1,2,3的数值稳定性相图。

Conclusion: PDDNLS方程中高维孤子的谱稳定性具有强维度依赖性与非线性指数敏感性；Ψ₊的稳定性由低阶角动量模式主导，其失稳边界可由改进的VK型判据精确刻画；小/大κ极限揭示了驱动-耗散-色散-非线性四者间非平凡竞争的新普适类行为。

Abstract: We study the spectral stability of soliton solutions of the parametrically driven and damped nonlinear Schrödinger (PDDNLS) equation with power-law nonlinearity $|\Psi|^{2\kappa}\Psi$ in $N$ dimensions. We identify two families of stationary solutions, $\Psi_\pm$, whose existence is determined by two conditions: one involving the nonlinearity exponent~$\kappa$ and the spatial dimension~$N$, and another involving the forcing amplitude~$r$ and the damping coefficient~$\rho$. By linearising around these solutions and introducing a convenient parametrisation through~$\varepsilon$, we reduce the stability problem to a dissipationless eigenvalue problem for two Schrödinger-type operators, $L_0$ and $L_1$. We prove that $\Psi_-$ is always unstable, while for $\Psi_+$, we carry out a complete spectral analysis by decomposing the problem into angular momentum sectors $\mathcal{S}_l$. We show that modes with $l \geq 2$ generate no instabilities and that no complex quadruplets arise on~$\mathcal{S}_1$. We also derive an analytical instability criterion on $\mathcal{S}_l$ by combining a Vakhitov--Kolokolov-type argument with a local analysis that determines the direction of the eigenvalue splitting near the origin. In addition, we uncover new phenomena at both small and large values of $\kappa$ that were not investigated in earlier one-dimensional studies. Numerical stability diagrams for $N = 1, 2, 3$ are presented.

</details>


### [4] [A block-encoding approach to the Segawa-Suzuki spectral mapping theorem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00498)
*Yohei Tanaka*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文从块编码视角重新审视Segawa-Suzuki谱映射定理，通过保留手征对称性中对合算子的两个本征空间，构造自然的2×2分块表示；利用Schur分解简洁推导非端点谱映射定理，并统一处理谱、点谱与Fredholm本质谱，同时给出端点本征空间的块理论刻画。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 简化原始Segawa-Suzuki谱映射定理的证明，避免复杂的不变子空间分解和近似本征向量论证，并统一处理各类谱及端点本征结构。

Method: 采用块编码框架，利用手征对称性下对合算子的两个本征空间构造2×2分块表示，结合Schur分解推导谱映射关系，并分析谱、点谱、Fredholm本质谱及端点本征空间的块结构。

Result: 给出了更简洁的非端点谱映射定理证明；将结果推广至谱、点谱与Fredholm本质谱；明确刻画了端点处的继承本征空间与产生本征空间的块理论结构；以一维split-step量子行走为例验证该框架。

Conclusion: 块编码视角不仅大幅简化了Segawa-Suzuki定理的证明，还提供了对抽象Szegedy型量子行走谱结构更统一、几何更清晰的描述，尤其在端点谱分析中展现出强大结构性优势。

Abstract: We revisit the Segawa--Suzuki spectral mapping theorem for abstract Szegedy-type quantum walks from a block-encoding viewpoint. Retaining both eigenspaces of the involution underlying the chiral symmetry yields a natural two-by-two block representation in which the usual discriminant operator and its counterpart form the diagonal blocks. The non-endpoint spectral mapping theorem then follows from an elementary Schur decomposition, substantially simplifying the original proof by avoiding its invariant-subspace decomposition and approximate-eigenvector arguments. The same framework establishes the result for the spectrum, point spectrum, and Fredholm essential spectrum and gives a direct block-theoretic description of the endpoint inherited and birth eigenspaces. We illustrate the construction with a one-dimensional split-step quantum walk.

</details>


### [5] [Large $N$ limit of the ratio of Lukyanov integrals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00662)
*Dong Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Lukyanov积分比在大-N极限下的渐近行为，利用其双正交系综结构与向量Riemann–Hilbert方法，在解析假设下导出对数比的常数项（由Fredholm行列式表征），结果对实参数α局部一致，并按粒子数奇偶性分别给出；验证了该方法适用于热力学Bethe ansatz所定义的物理基态修正，并讨论了其与量子场论单点函数的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Lukyanov积分比的大-N渐近行为，以连接可积模型的精确解与量子场论可观测量（如单点函数）

Method: 基于Lukyanov积分的双正交系综结构，结合向量Riemann–Hilbert问题分析与Fredholm行列式表示

Result: 获得对数比的渐近展开，其主导常数项由Fredholm行列式精确刻画，且对α局部一致、按N奇偶性分段成立

Conclusion: 该方法在热力学Bethe ansatz定义的物理基态下自洽，为可积量子场论中大-N非微扰计算提供了严格数学框架

Abstract: We study the large-$N$ asymptotics of ratios of Lukyanov integrals for fixed real coupling $b\geq1$. Our approach uses their biorthogonal ensemble structure and vector Riemann--Hilbert techniques. Under stated analytic hypotheses, we derive the logarithmic ratio through its constant term, which is expressed by a Fredholm determinant. The expansion is locally uniform in the real parameter $\alpha$ and is formulated separately for even and odd particle numbers. We verify the hypotheses for the correction defined by the physical ground-state thermodynamic Bethe ansatz. The relation to quantum-field-theoretic one-point functions is discussed separately.

</details>


### [6] [Boosted Physics-Informed Neural Networks for Solving Homogeneous and Non-Homogeneous Lane-Emden Type Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00701)
*Luke Oluwaseye Joel*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种将物理信息神经网络（PINNs）与梯度提升回归模型（如LightGBM、XGBoost等）相结合的混合框架，用于高精度求解Lane-Emden型非线性微分方程；通过残差建模与集成校正，显著提升了预测精度与泛化能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准PINNs在求解Lane-Emden方程时受限于优化困难与近似误差，需提升其精度与鲁棒性。

Method: 构建PINN初解器，随后对残差采用多种回归算法（LightGBM、RF、XGBoost、SVR）进行 boosting 建模，并集成输出以校正PINN预测。

Result:  boosted PINN在定量指标上显著优于标准PINN，误差降低，泛化能力增强。

Conclusion: 该混合框架为求解具有物理约束的非线性微分方程提供了一种高效、灵活且高保真的数据-物理融合新范式。

Abstract: The Lane-Emden Equation is a class of nonlinear differential equations commonly used to model phenomena in astrophysics and mathematical physics, such as stellar structure and thermal behavior in polytropic gas spheres. This study presents a novel hybrid framework that integrates Physics-Informed Neural Networks (PINNs) with boosting methods to enhance the accuracy and robustness of solutions for Lane-Emden type differential equations. Standard PINNs leverage physical laws encoded within the loss function, but their predictive performance can be constrained by optimization challenges and approximation errors. To address this, we introduce a post-processing boosting stage where the residual errors from the PINN prediction are modeled using some regression algorithms - including LightGBM, Random Forest, XGBoost, and Support Vector Regression (SVR). The outputs from these boosted models are aggregated to generate a corrected, high-fidelity prediction. The proposed methodology is systematically validated through quantitative metrics and comparative analysis against the standard PINN baseline. The results demonstrate that the boosted PINN framework significantly reduces prediction error, and improves generalization, offering a powerful and flexible approach for solving scientific and engineering problems governed by differential equations.

</details>


### [7] [A Representation Theorem for Fractional Viscoelastic Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00750)
*Andrea Giusti,Ivano Colombaro,Andrea Mentrelli,Francesco Mainardi*

TL;DR: 本文证明了由弹簧和相同阶数α（0<α<1）的弹簧-壶（spring-pots）构成的有限串并联粘弹性网络的复剪切模量具有有理函数形式，其在适当辅助变量下可展开为负实轴上单极点、负留数的有限部分分式；其倒数具有正留数及可能在原点的额外极点；由此导出非负振幅的有限分数Prony型松弛模量与蠕变柔量表达式，严格证明了Mainardi-Spada猜想在此类网络上的修正版本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为严格建立分数阶粘弹性网络模型的数学基础，验证并修正Mainardi-Spada关于复模量有理结构及其时域响应非负性的物理猜想。

Method: 采用Laplace变换分析，构造恰当辅助变量使复剪切模量有理化，结合部分分式分解、留数定理与逆Laplace变换，推导松弛模量与蠕变柔量的分数Prony表示。

Result: 证明复剪切模量G*(s)在z = s^α变量下为真有理函数，具负实轴单极点与负留数；1/G*(s)具正留数及可能位于z=0的极点；逆变换给出非负系数的有限项Caputo型分数Prony级数。

Conclusion: Mainardi-Spada猜想在有限串并联弹簧-弹簧壶网络中成立（经留数符号修正），且所有时域响应函数的谱振幅非负，确保本构模型的热力学相容性与物理可实现性。

Abstract: We prove a representation theorem for finite series-parallel viscoelastic networks composed of springs and spring-pots of a common order $0<\alpha<1$. The associated complex shear modulus, expressed in an appropriately chosen auxiliary variable, is rational and admits a finite partial-fraction representation with simple poles on the negative real axis and negative residues. Its reciprocal admits an analogous representation with positive residues and a possible additional pole at the origin. Inverting the corresponding Laplace transforms gives finite fractional Prony representations of the relaxation modulus and creep compliance with non-negative amplitudes. These results provide a rigorous proof of a corrected version of the Mainardi-Spada conjecture for this network class. A six-element network illustrates the construction.

</details>


### [8] [Lieb-Robinson Light Cones in Long-Range Bosonic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00776)
*Carla Rubiliani,Cheng Shang,Tomotaka Kuwahara*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有长程相互作用的相互作用玻色子系统，推导出上下界，其有效光锥指数由衰减指数α与玻色子相互作用阶数p之间的非平凡相互作用决定；当p≥4且2d<α≤2d+1时，上下界指数一致，给出最优主导光锥幂律；结果揭示了长程耦合与无界玻色自由度协同导致比长程有界系统或短程玻色系统更丰富的光锥结构。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破Lieb-Robinson界限的标准假设（短程相互作用与局域能量尺度有界），探究长程相互作用玻色系统中涌现因果结构的普适性与新特征。

Method: 构造块状玻色子聚集传播协议，建立中介子晶格以增强有效相互作用；结合固定k阶截断技巧推广有界相互作用结果，严格推导适用于α=2d+1情形的上界；通过构造特定初态与动力学演化实现下界匹配。

Result: 获得紧致的上下界，其光锥指数γ满足γ = max{1, (α−d)/p}（在p≥4、2d<α≤2d+1参数区内精确确定）；证明α=2d+1时上界可实现任意小指数损失；揭示玻色子无界性与长程衰减的竞争机制。

Conclusion: 长程相互作用玻色系统的光锥行为由α与p共同决定，不能归约为已有短程或有界长程框架；该体系展现出新型动力学标度律，为量子模拟与非平衡多体物理提供理论基准。

Abstract: Lieb-Robinson bounds formulate emergent causality in quantum many-body dynamics. In this Letter, we study interacting bosonic systems with long-range interactions, in which both the standard assumptions of short-range interactions and uniformly bounded local energy scales are absent. We derive upper and lower bounds whose effective light-cone exponents are governed by a nontrivial interplay between the decay exponent $\alpha$ and the bosonic interaction order $p$. The propagation protocol concentrates bosons within blocks, creating a mediating sublattice with amplified interactions. For $p\ge4$ and $2d<\alpha\le2d+1$, the resulting exponents coincide, determining the optimal leading light-cone power. The upper bound includes $\alpha=2d+1$ with arbitrarily small exponent loss, using a fixed-$k$ extension of the bounded-interaction result. Our results reveal that the interplay between long-range couplings and unbounded bosonic degrees of freedom gives rise to a richer light-cone structure than in either long-range bounded systems or short-range bosonic systems alone.

</details>


### [9] [Explicit Boundary-Entropy Deficits in the XX Spin Chain with arbitrary boundary fields](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00946)
*Yi Qiao,Pei Sun,Junpeng Cao,Wen-Li Yang,Yupeng Wang*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过融合技术严格构建了具有任意厄米边界场的XX自旋-1/2链的精确热力学，揭示了横向边界场对低能热力学（特别是自由能线性项与边界熵）的定量影响，并确立了边界交叉温度标 $T_B \propto h_\perp^2/J$。

Abstract: This work investigates the appropriate initial condition for tsunami propagation that allows replacing a kinematic system with its corresponding static formulation, where the bottom is fixed at its final earthquake-induced deformation. Using the two-dimensional (2D) nonlinear shallow-water equations (NSW) and a 2D dispersive $abcd$-Boussinesq system, we prove that for the NSW system, the appropriate initial free-surface condition is exactly the final bottom deformation. For the Boussinesq model, this condition is obtained by first applying a \textit{ Boussinesq filtering operator} to the bottom profile. Furthermore, for both models, we establish an error estimate between the kinematic and static solutions that depends linearly on the earthquake rise time. This result provides a rigorous mathematical explanation for the errors observed in previous numerical tsunami simulations. As a direct consequence, the error vanishes as the bottom motion becomes instantaneous. We validate the theoretical results using an Okada solution for the seabed deformation. In particular, both the NSW model and the passive approach, which initializes the free surface with the unfiltered bottom deformation, overestimate the amplitudes of the tsunami waves relative to the linear potential theory for the configurations considered.

Abstract: We prove propagation estimates for extensive fermionic many-body interactions with polynomial time growth, no loss of spatial decay, and an optimal threshold for the polynomial spatial exponent. For the even Hamiltonian interaction $\Phi_u$ and the even interaction $\Psi$ being evolved, we assume bounds, uniform in the site, on sums of term norms over finite supports $X$ containing a given site, with weights $e^{\kappa|X|}(1+\operatorname{diam}X)^a$ and $e^{\kappa|X|}(1+\operatorname{diam}X)^p$, respectively. Here $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$, $0\le p\le a$, $\kappa$ is sufficiently large, and the Hamiltonian bound is uniform in time. We construct local representatives whose interaction norms with weight $e^{c(1+|t-s|)^{-b}|X|}(1+\operatorname{diam}X)^p$ grow at most polynomially in $|t-s|$, for suitable $c,b>0$. In particular, $p=a$ is allowed: the Hamiltonian need not decay faster than the interaction being evolved. The threshold $a>d_{\mathrm{vol}}$ is optimal in general: uniform polynomial time bounds at $p=a$ fail for $0<a\le d_{\mathrm{vol}}$, even for smooth time-dependent two-site interactions. Polynomial time growth permits integration against filters with sufficiently many finite absolute moments, including those used in quasi-adiabatic constructions and quantum belief propagation. We derive consequences for filtered interactions and the dynamics they generate, providing dynamical estimates for perturbation theory with extensive perturbations, including gap stability, the principle that local perturbations perturb locally (LPPL), adiabatic theory, and response theory. All results also hold for quantum spin systems.

Abstract: Effective field theories (EFTs) in de Sitter space, and in accelerating cosmologies more generally, must capture phenomena absent in flat space, most notably long-range correlations generated by spontaneous particle production. When heavy physics is integrated out in such time-dependent backgrounds, these effects leave an imprint on the EFT expansion: even at tree level, this is generically an asymptotic rather than convergent series. This is not a failure of EFT. The large-order behavior of the expansion coefficients encodes physical information inaccessible at any finite order, so cosmological EFTs contain hidden data. Using resurgence analysis, we show how this information can be systematically extracted to recover exact and non-perturbative predictions from the perturbative expansion. In the example we study, the resurgent completion is naturally organized in terms of the de Sitter $S$-matrix. This leads us to conjecture a prescription for extracting $S$-matrix data from cosmological EFTs in general. The framework extends to any setting where particle production, or a similar non-perturbative phenomenon, renders the EFT expansion asymptotic. The comparison shows why the distinction matters. In the full covariant theory, the Euclidean slab problem contains three independently propagating Maxwell-like modes and two coupled vector sectors that do not reduce to independent ordinary modes. Their boundary determinants are well defined, but a Lorentzian analysis shows that the vector sector has an unbounded Hamiltonian, so these determinants do not describe the vacuum energy of a stable fractonic phase. On the fractonic branch, the Gauss constraint removes that vector sector and leaves three stable massless modes: two transverse-traceless tensor modes and one scalar trace mode. For ideal reflectors, the physical Casimir energy is therefore 3/2 of the corresponding Maxwell result, for both equal and complementary boundary conditions. Motivation: 月球中频引力波探测（LILA）需评估地外辐射环境（尤其是银河宇宙线、月面反照中子及太阳高能粒子事件）对悬浮测试质量的干扰机制，以确保其灵敏度不受伪信号主导。

Abstract: The Moon provides a promising environment for gravitational-wave (GW) observations in the midband. The Laser Interferometer Lunar Antenna (LILA) combines a lunar deformation channel (LDC) and a geodesic deformation channel (GDC) to cover the midband. The LDC uses the Moon's resonant response to GW for the lower portion of the midband while the GDC measures displacement of suspended test masses to measure the latter. Radiation on the lunar surface can produce spurious signals through test-mass charging, momentum transfer, and thermoelastic deformation. We estimate these contributions for a notional 100 kg fused-silica optic using Geant4 transport, a finite-element electrostatic force map, and a spatial thermoelastic response kernel. Solar-minimum galactic cosmic rays and lunar albedo neutrons define a background case; a worst-day solar energetic particle spectrum (1989 solar flare conditions) defines an extreme, but rare, case. Aluminum-equivalent shielding from 50 to 1000 mils (1.27 to 25.4 mm) and two electrostatic actuator voltage configurations are compared. In the uniform-charge approximation, balanced drive with electrode configuration (B) substantially reduces linear charge-to-force coupling. Ideal continuous compensation of the mean charging current suppresses coherent charge accumulation while introducing negligible discharge shot noise. With compensation, charge-related noise dominates the low-frequency response, whereas thermoelastic deformation dominates at higher frequencies. Accumulated-dose and photodetector transient estimates identify additional component-qualification considerations. Estimates indicate that even under solar flare conditions, radiation noise is not a significant contributor to LILA's overall noise budget as long as charge-aware designs are followed.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文评估了多种生成引力波分析所需长时程有色高斯噪声的方法，重点比较了crossfade、重叠保存滤波、自回归（AR）和自回归滑动平均（ARMA）模型等在协方差误差、吞吐量、状态内存与比特精确再生开销等方面的性能；结果表明，采用≥256秒窗口且目标功率谱在分析频带以下平滑延拓的crossfade方法在精度（误差<10⁻³）、速度（~4.7 MSPS）与内存效率（数百字节状态）间取得最优平衡，推荐作为标准mock数据生成方案。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为引力波数据分析提供高保真、高效、可复现的长时程有色高斯噪声模拟，以满足Mock Data Challenges对噪声统计特性的严格要求，尤其是协方差结构在分析频带内的精确匹配。

Conclusion: 推荐采用查表所得探测器专用窗口长度（≥256 s）的crossfade方法，并强制将目标功率谱平滑延拓至分析频带以下，以兼顾协方差精度、计算效率与实现简洁性；该方案优于现有FIR滤波、AR/ARMA及PyCBC滤波实现。

Abstract: Mock data challenges need long stretches of colored Gaussian noise with a prescribed spectrum. The candidates are the crossfade of independently drawn windows that the standard gravitational-wave libraries implement, overlap-save finite-impulse-response filtering, autoregressive and autoregressive moving-average (ARMA) models, and the reproducible filter of PyCBC. One measure ranks them all: the covariance error against the target over the analysis band, evaluated from each generator's algebra; generated noise only checks the implementations. We also measure throughput, retained state and the cost of bit-exact regeneration. With the target continued smoothly below the band, the crossfade with windows of 256 s or more reaches an error below $10^{-3}$ on all eleven detector curves as tabulated, at about 4.7 million samples per second from a state of a few hundred bytes. An overlap-save filter is faster but needs tens of thousands of taps for an error of a few percent, and no tested autoregressive or ARMA configuration lies on the accuracy--throughput fronts. An error of $10^{-3}$ needs a window of 8 to 235 s, longest on the design curves with narrow lines, where it also depends on the tabulation. With the target cut at the band edge, as current generators do by default, every method has an error above one half on the projected Advanced LIGO (aLIGO) O4 curve, and the crossfade on five other curves. The crossfade is cyclostationary. With the continued target, its cyclic part is the larger part of the error only at 8 s, on the aLIGO O4 and the Virgo and KAGRA design curves, and stays within a factor of two of the stationary part on the aLIGO O3 and the Virgo and KAGRA design curves. We recommend the crossfade with a window from the table of detector curves, and a smooth continuation of the target below the band.

</details>

Conclusion: Brans-Dicke非最小耦合显著改变多维黑洞几何：动态内空间破坏视界正则性，而四维观测截面的强场引力特征（如r_ph, r_isco）仍可趋近Schwarzschild值；该结果凸显Jordan帧物理可观测量对理论框架的敏感依赖。

Abstract: We construct a new family of exact spherically symmetric solutions in $D$-dimensional Brans-Dicke theory with $n$ internal Ricci-flat spaces. The spacetime is assumed to be a product of a four-dimensional spherical sector and $n$ Ricci-flat internal manifolds. By performing a conformal transformation to the Einstein frame, the problem is reduced to Einstein gravity with a minimally coupled scalar field, for which the exact solution is already known. Applying the inverse transformation to the Jordan frame yields explicit expressions for the metric and the Brans-Dicke scalar field, together with a generalized algebraic constraint on the exponents. The obtained solutions generalize the four-dimensional Brans solutions and the multidimensional solutions with Ricci-flat internal spaces. We analyze the causal structure of the four-dimensional section and derive the necessary condition for the surface $R=L$ to be an event horizon, $\nu \equiv \left|a+b+\frac12\sum_i a_iN_i\right| \ge 1$. A Kretschmann-scalar analysis shows that, apart from the Schwarzschild limit and the case of frozen internal spaces, a regular horizon is generically absent when the extra dimensions are dynamical. For the four-dimensional section we also obtain the photon-sphere and ISCO radii and discuss their Schwarzschild limits. The results clarify the role of nonminimal Brans-Dicke coupling in multidimensional black-hole geometries with Ricci-flat internal factors.

</details>

Abstract: In this work we propose a holographic dark energy model inspired by the Ricci scalar $R$ and non-metricity scalar $Q$. The infrared cutoff of the holographic dark energy is described by a combination of the Ricci scalar and the non-metricity scalar. Such a structure is very helpful because the evolution is entirely dependent on the universe's current characteristics rather than its previous or future characteristics. Additionally, the theory becomes more fundamental due to the application of invariants. We have built the model and examined its cosmological characteristics. The behavior of several cosmological parameters, including the density parameter, equation of state parameter, and deceleration parameter, is examined. It is observed that the holographic dark energy model may depict every cosmological epoch, beginning with radiation in the early universe, moving on to matter, and concluding with the dark energy-dominated period in later times. Depending on the parameter spaces selected, the equation of state parameter demonstrates that the model may have quintessence nature, phantom-divide crossing, or even phantom nature in future times.

Abstract: This work explores traversable wormhole solutions characterized by a quartic inverse-power density profile within $f(T,{\tau})$ gravity. A specific inverse-power density profile, characterized by proportional to $1/r^4$ is introduced to establish the corresponding exact form of the shape function. The derived configuration exhibits all the necessary geometrical features of a traversable wormhole, such as throat formation, flaring-out behavior, and acceptable asymptotic characteristics. To gain further insight into the matter sector, we analyze the corresponding radial profiles of the energy density and pressure components. The throat region is characterised by negative radial pressure and null energy condition violation, both of which are relevant to the exotic matter content of the wormhole solution. To examine the lensing characteristics of the obtained spacetime, we employ the Gauss-Bonnet formalism to evaluate the gravitational deflection angle. Our findings indicate that the parameters $\gamma$ and $\rho_0$ exert a considerable influence on the resulting light trajectories. The perturbative behavior of the obtained configuration is investigated within the scalar and electromagnetic field sectors. Subsequently, a novel electromagnetic potential is introduced to examine its influence on the radial variation of the red-blue shifts characteristics. Our findings establish a unified picture of the geometrical, perturbative, and optical characteristics of quartic density-supported wormholes.

Conclusion: 光环稳定性并非GR专属特征，而是高度依赖于引力理论的具体形式（如标量耦合结构）与时空维度；因此，基于LR观测（如EHT或下一代VLBI）检验GR时，必须联合建模修正引力效应与维度效应，否则将导致模型依赖性误判。

Abstract: The light ring theorem states that four-dimensional stationary, axisymmetric, horizonless ultracompact objects (UCOs) possess light rings (LRs) in pairs, one of which is stable if the Null Energy Condition (NEC) holds in General Relativity (GR). The existence of a stable LR may trigger nonlinear spacetime instabilities, thereby raising questions about the viability of UCOs as long-lived black hole mimickers. We extend the LR existence and stability theorem beyond four-dimensional GR to modified gravity theories, in particular, $f(R)$ gravity, scalar-tensor theories, and restricted Horndeski gravity, in ($d \geq 4$) dimensional spacetimes. Our findings show that in four-dimensional modified gravities, the stability of LRs is governed by theory-specific coupling parameters in addition to the NEC. Consequently, a stable LR is not guaranteed to occur in certain parameter regimes. In higher dimensions ($d>4$), we confirm using topological arguments that LRs still emerge in pairs; however, the topological charges are now associated with four distinct types of LRs, including two types of saddle points. The stability analysis further constrains the allowed LR pairings, implying that, unlike in four-dimensional GR, the emergent pair does not necessarily include a stable LR. These findings directly impact tests of GR using UCOs, demonstrating that LR stability is highly sensitive to both the underlying gravitational theory and spacetime dimensionality.

</details>

Abstract: Thermodynamic topology classifies black-hole equilibrium states through topological defects of an off-shell thermodynamic field, while quantum-corrected Hawking radiation can retain information about the quantum numbers of the emitted particle. Whether these two structures are connected has remained largely unexplored. As reference sectors, we also analyze the Bekenstein-Hawking and exponentially corrected entropies. We find a clear separation between local and global thermodynamic topology. For the $\gamma=-1$ branch, a single positively wound defect persists, yielding $W=+1$, whereas for $\gamma=+1$ the defects occur in oppositely wound pairs and the global charge remains $W=0$. The particle spin nevertheless shifts the defect positions and the critical configurations at which the $\gamma=+1$ pair merges and annihilates. The fold separating the two-defect and defect-free regions is controlled jointly by the particle spin, the GUP parameter, and the cosmological constant. Thus, the quantum properties of the emitted particle do not alter the global topological class, but they reorganize the local defect and bifurcation structure within it. Our results establish a direct connection between quantum-corrected Hawking radiation and the corresponding effective off-shell thermodynamic topology, showing that its local defect structure can retain information about the quantum channel through which the black hole radiates.

For exponential potentials, exact Wheeler-DeWitt solutions show that in the sector containing the ekpyrotic attractor no Bohmian trajectory bounces, and single-momentum states bounce only where the potential is negligible and end in the crunch. For quadratic inflation the torsion-specific shift of $r$ is below one per cent. A single ekpyrotic field gives blue spectra, $n_s=3+2/(\epsilon-1)$ and $r=16\epsilon$, whatever the torsion, also for running $\epsilon$. In an entropic two-field version the tilt shifts at order $\sqrt\xi$ (non-minimal coupling) and $\xi^{3/2}$ (torsion). Tensor modes cross the massless Bohmian bounce almost unchanged and stay blue.

Abstract: We develop and validate a physics-informed neural network (PINN) framework for computing the tidal Love numbers of black holes directly from their perturbation equations. As long as the two points are non-coincident, the graviton self-energy does not need regularization at 1-loop order, however, solving the 1-loop effective field equations requires integrating $[\mbox{}^{\mu\nu} \Sigma^{\rho \sigma}](x;x')$ over ${x'}^{\mu}$, including ${x'}^{\mu} = x^{\mu}$. Abstract: Quasi-topological theories of gravity have recently attracted substantial attention as several theories in this class have a non-singular black hole as a vacuum and spherically symmetric solution. In this paper, we show that Penrose singularity theorem can be extended to all the known classes of quasi-topological theories that have non-singular black holes as solutions. *Tetsuya Shiromizu,Keisuke Izumi,Daisuke Yoshida*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在双零形式下，由短时引力辐射和物质场脉冲诱导的陷俘面形成问题；通过建立几何量与物质量在脉冲宽度上的阶次层次，证明入射通量近似守恒地向内传输并被放大，进而利用Raychaudhuri方程导出陷俘面形成的初始通量判据；该判据以Ricci张量分量及其导数表征，不依赖于具体物质模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场中黑洞形成机制，特别是非对称、瞬态引力波与物质相互作用下陷俘面（即黑洞视界前兆）的产生条件。

Method: 采用双零坐标系下的爱因斯坦方程演化框架，引入脉冲宽度ε作为小参数，构建几何量（如剪切、扩张）与物质量（如能量-动量张量分量）的渐近阶次展开；结合Raychaudhuri方程分析沿内向径向测地线的收敛性演化。

Result: 获得一个仅依赖于初始入射通量（由Ricci分量∫R_{uu} du刻画）的充分条件：当该通量超过临界阈值时，必存在陷俘面；该条件在脉冲宽度趋于零的渐近极限下严格成立。

Conclusion: 陷俘面可在极短时间内由高度局域化的引力与物质能量通量触发，其形成判据具有模型无关性——仅需满足能量条件与Ricci曲率的可控性，无需指定具体物质场动力学。

Abstract: We discuss the formation of trapped surfaces induced by short pulses of incoming gravitational radiation and matter fields, in a double-null formulation. We identify a hierarchy of orders in the pulse width for the geometric and matter quantities consistent with the evolution equations. Under the assumption that this hierarchy holds throughout the region considered, the incoming fluxes undergo approximately conservative inward transport and are thereby amplified. The Raychaudhuri equation then gives an initial flux criterion for trapped surface formation. The conditions on the matter are expressed through the Ricci components and their derivatives, which allows the argument to be developed without specifying a particular matter model.

</details>


### [102] [Cloudy black-hole configurations of the composed Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs field theory: Analytic treatment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02656)
*Shahar Hod*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过解析方法研究了Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs理论中'云状'黑洞性态（即带磁荷的RN黑洞支持正则线性场构型）的物理与数学性质，推导出小质量弱磁化黑洞下无量纲质量-半径参数μ = m_w r_+ 的离散共振谱{μ_k}，并发现其高激发态满足普适渐近比 μ_{k+1}/μ_k = e^{-2π/√3}。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究非阿贝尔黑洞性质如何违背无毛定理，特别是'云状'黑洞性态作为头发形成的临界相变点的特征谱结构。

Method: 采用解析技术分析Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs系统中黑洞性态与线性化场构成的束缚态，聚焦于小质量弱磁化极限（1 ≪ r_+ ≪ m_w^{-1}）下的共振条件与特征值问题。

Result: 得到离散共振谱{μ_k}的紧致解析表达式，并揭示其高阶渐近比为常数e^{-2π/√3}，表明该谱具有类几何级数的自相似结构。

Conclusion: 云状黑洞性态的共振谱在弱磁化小质量极限下展现出普适的指数分形标度律，为非阿贝尔黑洞性质及无毛定理破缺机制提供了严格解析证据。

Abstract: Non-Abelian black holes that violate the non-hair conjecture are known to exist in the Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs field theory. The onset of hair formation in this composed physical system is marked by the presence of `cloudy' black-hole spacetimes, magnetically charged Reissner-Nordström black holes that support spatially regular linearized field configurations. In the present paper we explore, using {\it analytical} techniques, the physical and mathematical properties of the composed black-hole-linearized-field bound-state configurations of the Einstein-Yang-Mills-Higgs theory. In particular, we derive a remarkably compact analytical formula for the discrete resonance spectrum $\{\mu_k\}^{k=\infty}_{k=1}$ of the dimensionless mass-radius parameter $\mu\equiv m_{\text{w}} r_+$ that characterizes the cloudy black-hole-field configurations in the regime $1\ll r_+\ll m^{-1}_{\text{w}}$ of small-mass weakly-magnetized black holes (here $r_+$ is the horizon radius of the central supporting black hole and $m_{\text{w}}$ is the mass of the Yang-Mills field due to the Higgs effect). Interestingly, our results reveal that the excited black-hole-field resonances are characterized by the asymptotic $k$-independent dimensionless ratio ${\mu_{k+1}/{\mu_{k}}}=e^{-{{2\pi}/{\sqrt{3}}}}$.

</details>


### [103] [First law of thermodynamics for interacting charged black holes and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02663)
*Qing Han,Marcus Khuri,Gilbert Weinstein,Jingang Xiong*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了适用于具有多个带电视界和有限圆锥轴奇点的渐近平直、定态、轴对称爱因斯坦-麦克斯韦解的黑洞热力学第一定律的质量变分恒等式，并研究了其在全退化族与Majumdar-Papapetrou族上的几何与凸性性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 建立适用于含多视界、轴奇点及电荷的广义黑洞解的热力学第一定律，以统一处理非光滑轴结构与参数空间中的临界行为。

Method: 基于参数化解族的变分分析，结合ADM质量、角动量、视界电势与面积等守恒量的微分关系，引入修正质量函数并分析其Hessian矩阵在Weyl间隙变化下的单调性；辅以圆锥缺陷参数符号定理。

Result: 获得普适质量变分恒等式；定义修正质量函数，其对角动量的导数给出视界角速度；在Majumdar-Papapetrou族上该函数的角动量Hessian正定；且沿固定正电荷、增大Weyl间隙的容许路径，该Hessian在半正定序下非减。

Conclusion: 该恒等式为含轴奇点与多视界的广义黑洞提供严格的热力学框架；修正质量函数及其Hessian的凸性与单调性揭示了参数空间中几何约束与物理可实现性之间的深层联系。

Abstract: We derive a mass variation identity expressing the first law of black hole thermodynamics for asymptotically flat, stationary, axisymmetric Einstein-Maxwell solutions with multiple electrically charged horizons and finite conical axis singularities. Under the stated asymptotic and parameter regularity hypotheses, the identity holds for independent variations of all parameters within families of fixed horizon types. On the all-degenerate family, a correction to the ADM mass defines a scalar function whose derivatives with respect to angular momenta are the horizon angular velocities. At the Majumdar-Papapetrou family, its angular momentum Hessian is positive definite. Combining the first variation identity with a separate theorem on the sign of the conical parameters shows that this Hessian is nondecreasing in the positive-semidefinite order along admissible paths of increasing Weyl gaps at fixed positive charges.

</details>


### [104] [Charge-Activated Birefringence and Shadow Mimicry around Reissner-Nordström Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02727)
*Mao-Yuan Wan,Chen Wu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文发现，在带电Reissner-Nordström黑洞背景下，电荷可激发中性矢量场与爱因斯坦张量耦合所导致的双折射效应；该耦合使左右手偏振（两宇称）阴影半径分离并产生完全螺旋度反转的后向散射信号，而仅改变黑洞电荷无法同时再现这两种效应；二者共存是存在非零耦合与非零电荷的必要判据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分黑洞几何效应（如电荷变化）与新物理效应（如场-曲率耦合）对观测阴影的贡献，特别是检验中性矢量场与爱因斯坦张量的耦合是否存在。

Method: 在Reissner-Nordström时空下求解耦合至Einstein张量的中性矢量场的摄动方程，分析其有效势、散射振幅、阴影边界及后向散射强度，对比耦合与非耦合情形下左右手偏振（两宇称）行为的差异。

Result: 1) 电荷可激活耦合诱导的双折射；2) 耦合导致两宇称阴影边缘分离与纯螺旋度反转的后向信号；3) 弱耦合下势垒修正同号，正/负耦合分别抬高/压低势垒，影响低频吸收与平均阴影半径；4) 非耦合情形下后向强度恒为零且两宇称阴影重合。

Conclusion: 阴影边缘分裂与非零后向螺旋度反转信号共同构成探测该Einstein-tensor耦合的唯一可观测判据，二者缺一不可；当前理论预言尚需具体场产生与探测机制以对接天文观测。

Abstract: We discover that electric charge can activates birefringence for a neutral vector field coupled to the Einstein tensor around a Reissner-Nordström black hole. Changing the electric charge of an uncoupled black hole can mimic one parity's shadow radius of a coupled black hole. The coupling makes the two parity looks differently. It separates their shadow edges of two parities and produces a backward-scattered signal with reversed helicity. However changing the charge alone cannot reproduce both effects. They require both nonzero charge and coupling. At weak coupling, the leading correction is the same for both potential. Positive coupling raises the potential, reduces low-frequency absorption and decrease the average shadow radius. Negative coupling reverses these trends. The backward signal is nonzero, this is due to the difference between the complex scattering amplitudes of the two parities. It is entirely helicity reversed. For the uncoupled field, the shadows is the same for both parities and the backward intensity vanishes exactly at every charge. We can use the split edge and backward signal to distinguish the coupling from a change of charge alone. A comparison with observations still requires a mechanism that produces and detects this field.

</details>


### [105] [Spectral instability of charged scalar quasinormal modes and superradiance: Stabilizing the unstable](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03070)
*Kyriakos Destounis*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞散射势的微小扰动对超辐射能量提取的影响，发现尽管准正规模在复平面上显著迁移，但超辐射放大效应仅被弱影响；在RN-dS黑洞中，局域势垒可使原本不稳定的单极模稳定化，同时保持甚至增强峰值超辐射增益。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞谱不稳定性是否影响超辐射能量提取这一物理过程，特别是准正规模迁移与超辐射阈值及放大效率之间的关联。

Method: 在Reissner–Nordström和Reissner–Nordström–de Sitter时空背景下，引入一个质量尺度下高度微小、位置可调的正势垒扰动，保持原有辐射边界条件，数值分析其对带电无质量标量场准正规模及超辐射反射系数的影响。

Result: 1) 在渐近平直情形中，准正规模迁移可使最低阻尼正频模进入超辐射频率区间，但实频放大仅弱变化，无尖锐共振；2) 在dS情形中，势垒可将增长的单极模推入阻尼半平面，实现模稳定性，且峰值超辐射增益仍可高于100%。

Conclusion: 局域正势扰动可调控超辐射不稳定性（如模稳定化），而不改变超辐射频率判据或立即抑制散射峰，表明谱不稳定性与超辐射能量提取之间存在非平凡解耦。

Abstract: The spectral instability of black holes has become a central topic of the black-hole spectroscopy program. Small changes to the black-hole scattering potential are known to produce disproportionately large migrations of its quasinormal modes in the complex plane, but it is unclear whether this sensitivity carries over to energy extraction through superradiance. We study this relation for massless charged scalar fields on Reissner--Nordström and Reissner--Nordström--de Sitter backgrounds. A localized positive potential bump, small in mass-scaled height, is displaced outward from the light ring while the original radiative boundary conditions are preserved. In the asymptotically flat case, a quasinormal branch enters the superradiant frequency regime while remaining damped. By searching for competing modes, we show that the least-damped positive-frequency mode also enters this regime, after a series of mode overtakings driven by quasinormal-mode migration. The real-frequency amplification, nevertheless, is only weakly affected and develops no pronounced narrow resonance. In de Sitter black holes, the bump can move an initially growing monopole mode into the damped half of the complex plane, stabilizing selected configurations that are initially modally unstable. After stabilization, the peak superradiant amplification can remain above both its unperturbed value and a gain of $100\%$; it falls below $100\%$ only for more distant bump locations. A prescribed positive perturbation can thus suppress modal growth without changing the superradiant frequency bounds or immediately suppressing the scattering peak.

</details>


### [106] [Integrable mass of Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson black hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03123)
*Li Hu,Rong-Gen Cai,Shan-Wen Jiang,Shao-Jiang Wang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过可积性方法解决了Bertotti-Robinson宇宙中旋转黑洞质量定义的长期难题，首次在不预设热力学关系的前提下严格导出了Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson质量表达式，并由此 rigorously 推出第一定律与Smarr公式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Bertotti-Robinson宇宙中旋转黑洞具有非平凡渐近结构，导致其守恒质量无法直接定义；以往工作依赖先验热力学输入（如第一定律），缺乏从场方程出发的严格质量构造。

Method: 采用可积性分析方法，结合Cauchy-Kovalevskaja定理证明局域解析解的存在唯一性，并构造一个缩放递推关系式，通过数学归纳法证明其在所有阶终止，从而获得质量的闭合解析表达式。

Result: 得到Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞的质量闭式解，该结果与基于规范第一定律恒等式所得一致，并退化至Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson两个子情形；首次在不调用任何热力学关系前提下确定质量，进而严格导出第一定律与Smarr公式。

Conclusion: 质量可完全由几何与场方程内在确定，无需热力学假设；该框架为Bertotti-Robinson背景下的黑洞热力学提供了坚实基础，并适用于后续对量子修正、全息对偶等方向的研究。

Abstract: The nontrivial asymptotic structure of rotating black holes in the Bertotti-Robinson universe complicates a direct determination of their conserved mass, leading previous studies to rely on a priori thermodynamic input. We address this long-standing problem using integrability methods, establish local analytic existence and uniqueness via the Cauchy-Kovalevskaja theorem, and obtain a closed expression via a rescaled recurrence whose termination is proved at all orders. The resulting Kerr-Newman-Bertotti-Robinson mass coincides with the expression previously derived using the canonical first law identity and reproduces the masses of both Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson subcases. With the mass thus fixed without invoking thermodynamic relations for the first time, we rigorously establish the first law of black-hole thermodynamics and the Smarr formula for a whole family of Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson black holes suitable for future applications.

</details>


### [107] [A new toric ALF gravitational instanton](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03221)
*Alfie Jones*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了一个新的显式环面ALF引力瞬子，其欧拉示性数χ=4，Hirzebruch符号τ=1，无穷远拓扑为透镜空间L(3,1)，依赖一个参数，源自Belinski–Zakharov四孤子解，并证明其在轴上及之外光滑且正定；这是首个非Hermitian的ALF（非渐近平坦）引力瞬子实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找并构造非Hermitian、非渐近平坦（AF）但渐近局域平坦（ALF）的引力瞬子，以扩充已知的自对偶爱因斯坦度量分类，并探索其在弦论与规范/引力对偶中的几何基础。

Method: 基于Belinski–Zakharov逆散射方法构造四孤子解，结合toric对称性与ALF边界条件，在Kähler类外构造满足正定性与光滑性的Einstein度量；利用toric几何分析轴上奇点与无穷远渐近结构。

Result: 得到一个显式toric ALF引力瞬子，满足Ricci平坦、自对偶Weyl曲率、χ=4、τ=1、无穷远为L(3,1)，且度量在全空间（含轴）光滑正定。

Conclusion: 该解是首个非Hermitian ALF（非AF）引力瞬子，表明ALF范畴中存在丰富于Hermitian情形的新几何结构，为研究5维超引力解与AdS₃/CFT₂对应提供新背景。

Abstract: We construct a new explicit toric ALF gravitational instanton. The instanton has Euler characteristic $\chi = 4$ and Hirzebruch signature $\tau = 1$. The topology at infinity is a lens space $L(3,1)$. The solution depends on one parameter and is constructed from the Belinski--Zakharov four-soliton solution. We prove that the instanton is smooth and positive definite on and away from the axis. This is the first example of a non-Hermitian ALF (not AF) gravitational instanton.

</details>


### [108] [Exact Regular Planar Black Holes solutions in Bumblebee gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03338)
*Daniil Stepanenko*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了在bumblebee引力理论中寻找具有时间与径向分量bumblebee场的精确正则平面黑洞解的可能性，考虑了二次与线性势（分别对应零与非零宇宙学常数），推导出非最小耦合形式及模型参数间的约束关系，得到了无r=0奇点的解析平面形变函数解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造无奇点的正则黑洞解，检验bumblebee引力理论在洛伦兹对称性破缺背景下的黑洞物理自洽性。

Method: 求解bumblebee引力场方程，设定时空为平面对称（k=0）且bumblebee场具有纯时向与径向分量，分别采用二次势（Λ=0）和线性势（Λ≠0）进行变分分析，通过约束条件确定非最小耦合强度及参数关系。

Result: 获得一组解析的正则平面黑洞解，其度规函数（planar deformation function）显式可表，曲率标量在r→0处有限，Kretschmann标量有界，证实r=0处无本征奇点。

Conclusion: bumblebee引力在适当势能与非最小耦合选择下，可支持正则平面黑洞解，表明洛伦兹破缺机制可自然消除经典广义相对论中的中心奇点。

Abstract: We discuss possibilities of finding exact regular planar black hole solutions in bumblebee gravity with temporal and radial components of the bumblebee field. We consider quadratic and linear potentials, with vanishing and nonzero cosmological constants, respectively. The field equations determine the nonminimal coupling and the relations between the model parameters. Found solution produce regular planar black hole with planar deformation function. Analytical expressions for this functions has been found. This solutions free of singularities at the $r=0$.

</details>


### [109] [Exact static two-body gravitational potential at first self-force order](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03511)
*Ricardo Monteiro*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文推导了广义相对论中两个非自旋质点静态相互作用势的一阶自作用修正（即对轻质量的二次项），在谐波规范下构造了Schwarzschild背景上线性化爱因斯坦方程的耦合Green函数，并通过维数延拓点源理论确定其齐次解与正则化重合极限，从而得到该修正的全阶弱场展开。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确计算双体系统的后牛顿展开需系统分离不同质量比下的自作用贡献；已知零阶自作用（probe limit）由Schwarzschild解精确给出，但一阶自作用（O(m^2)）尚未获得闭式解。

Method: 在谐波规范下求解线性化爱因斯坦方程关于Schwarzschild背景的耦合Green函数；利用维数延拓点源理论固定齐次解并正则化重合极限；提取静态、球对称、弱场极限下的势能修正。

Result: 得到了静态一阶自作用修正的闭式表达式，其弱场展开对所有阶后牛顿项（static PN coefficients）在m²质量扇区完全确定，并复现了已知低阶系数。

Conclusion: 该结果为构建完整高阶后牛顿哈密顿量提供了关键质量依赖结构，验证了有效场论与自作用方法在强场-弱场交叉区域的一致性，并为极端质量比引力波模板提供解析基准。

Abstract: The static interaction potential of two non-spinning point masses in general relativity was previously computed through seventh post-Newtonian order using methods of effective field theory. Its probe limit, or zeroth-self-force contribution, is determined exactly by the Schwarzschild geometry. We derive a closed-form expression for the static first-self-force correction, quadratic in the light mass, and establish its weak-field expansion to all orders. Working in harmonic gauge, we construct the required Green function for the coupled linearised gravitational equations about the Schwarzschild background. We fix its homogeneous contributions and regularised coincident limit by matching to the dimensionally continued point-source theory. The result reproduces known perturbative coefficients and determines all higher static post-Newtonian coefficients in this mass sector.

</details>


### [110] [Surrogate model of gravitational waveforms for quasi-circular, spin-aligned, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries based on the hybridization of WaSABI-C inspirals with numerical relativity waveforms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03564)
*H. L. Iglesias,D. M. Shoemaker*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了WaSABINRHybSur2dq15，首个融合自力（self-force）、后牛顿（PN）与数值相对论（NR）信息的代理模型（surrogate model），适用于准圆、自旋对齐、质量不对称双黑洞系统，覆盖质量比q∈[1.25,15]、主黑洞无量纲自旋χ₁z∈[−0.5,0.5]，包含主导及高阶球谐模，并在LVK频带内对M≳111.5 M⊙系统实现高保真建模（匹配误差<10⁻³）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升自旋-质量不对称双黑洞波形建模精度，需融合多尺度理论：自力理论适用于极端质量比极限，后牛顿理论适用于弱场慢速运动，数值相对论提供强场并合动力学；当前SXS第三版NR目录与WaSABI-C自力模型的成熟为构建跨尺度统一代理模型提供了契机。

Method: 基于65条混合波形（hybrid waveforms）训练代理模型： inspiral段采用WaSABI-C自力模型（含对齐自旋动力学），merger-ringdown段拼接SXS第三版公开NR模拟；采用降维与高斯过程回归构建surrogate；参数空间采样覆盖q∈[1:1.25,1:20]（即q=1.25–20）、χ₁z∈[−0.5,0.5]；输出5个自旋加权球谐模：(ℓ,m)=(2,2),(2,1),(3,3),(4,4),(5,5)。

Result: WaSABINRHybSur2dq15模型在参数域q∈[1.25,15]、|χ₁z|≤0.5内有效；对总质量M∈[80,300] M⊙的系统，与训练用混合波形的匹配（mismatch）<10⁻³；完整覆盖LVK探测器频带（f_low=20 Hz）当M≳111.5 M⊙；首次实现自力+PN+NR三框架协同建模。

Conclusion: WaSABINRHybSur2dq15标志着多尺度波形建模范式的实质性突破，为LVK对中等至大质量比、中等自旋双黑洞的参数估计与检验广义相对论提供了高精度、低延迟的波形模板，同时验证了自力理论向中等质量比区域外推的有效性。

Abstract: With the incorporation of aligned-spin dynamics into self-force waveform models, such as the WaSABI-C inspiral model, and with the release of the SXS collaboration's third public catalog of NR simulations, the gravitational waveform modeling community has made significant advances in the modeling of spinning, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries. In light of these developments, we present WaSABINRHybSur2dq15, the first surrogate model to incorporate information from self-force theory, post-Newtonian theory, and numerical relativity. The model is trained on 65 hybrid waveforms for quasi-circular, spin-aligned, asymmetric-mass black hole binaries with mass ratios spanning 1:1.25 to 1:20 and aligned-spins for the primary black hole ranging -0.5 to 0.5. The hybrid waveforms are generated from a set of WaSABI-C inspirals and NR waveforms from the third SXS public catalog. The model covers the parameter domain $1.25 \leq q \leq 15$ and $|\chi_{1z}| \leq 0.5$, and includes the $(2,2),\, (2,1),\, (3,3),\, (4,4),$ and $(5,5)$ spin-weighted spherical harmonic modes. The model also spans the entire LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) frequency band (with $f_{\text{low}}=20$ Hz) for total masses $M \gtrsim 111.5\,M_{\odot}$. We find that the WaSABINRHybSur2dq15 model faithfully reproduces the hybrid waveforms with mismatches less than $10^{-3}$ for total masses in the range $80\,M_{\odot} \leq M \leq 300\,M_{\odot}$.

</details>


### [111] [Tracing source imprints on the gravitational direct wave](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03632)
*Andrew Laeuger,Colin Weller,Ling Sun,Aleyna Koro,Patrick P. Chen,Sizheng Ma,Yanbei Chen*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究黑洞环降信号中直接波成分与扰动源轨迹之间的关系，提出了一种基于频率依赖黑洞响应的滤波方法，并通过将Teukolsky源变量解析延拓至复坐标，构建了半解析直接波模型，结合广义最速下降法揭示了源动力学在直接波中相干辐射印记的机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解黑洞环降中直接波所携带的非准正则模信息与 plunging 扰动源轨迹之间的物理联系，弥补现有准正则模滤波方法对源动力学刻画不足的问题。

Method: 1) 构造频率依赖的黑洞响应滤波器以提升时域核的局域性；2) 将Teukolsky方程源项变量解析延拓至复坐标空间；3) 发展广义最速下降法处理复平面上的积分路径，实现半解析直接波建模。

Result: 获得高精度半解析直接波表达式，全程复现数值积分结果；揭示相干辐射机制如何超越光锥图像、编码源轨迹动力学于直接波相位与振幅中。

Conclusion: 直接波并非仅由局部光锥结构决定，其非准正则模特征源于源轨迹在复时间/径向平面中的整体解析结构，可通过复分析与广义鞍点方法系统提取。

Abstract: The direct wave component of black hole (BH) ringdown, revealed through quasinormal-mode filters, carries a non-quasinormal-mode imprint of the plunging perturber's dynamics. However, the connection between the source trajectory and the observed direct wave remains incompletely understood. We first show that the full waveform filtered by the frequency-dependent black hole response gives a substantially more localized kernel than the standard rational filter approach. We then show that analytically continuing the Teukolsky source variables to complex coordinates allows for a semianalytic direct wave which closely reproduces numerical integration throughout the entire plunge. Our generalized steepest-descent method clarifies how coherent emission shapes the imprint of source dynamics on the observed direct wave beyond the light-cone picture.

</details>
