Method: 采用夸克模型结合强衰变耦合计算（如^3P_0模型），区分λ-和ρ-激发模式，对1S/1P/1D波态进行质量谱预测与衰变宽度分析。

Result: 将多个实验观测态（Ξc(2790)–Ξc(3123)）系统地归属为Ξc/Ξc'的1P和1D波激发态，给出其具体量子数（J^P）、激发模式（λ/ρ）及主导衰变道（如Ξcπ, Ξc'π, ΛD, ΣD）。

Conclusion: 多数已观测Ξc激发态可被统一解释为轨道激发态；λ-模式主导低质量1P态，ρ-模式可能对应更高质量态；Λ/ΣD衰变道可用于区分Ξc'1D候选态；理论预言为后续BESIII/LHCb实验提供明确检验通道。

Abstract: Motivated by the rapidly accumulating experimental data, we systematically calculate the $\Xi_c$ and $\Xi_c'$ mass spectra for the $1S$-, $1P$-, and $1D$-wave states and investigate the strong decay properties of the $1P$- and $1D$-wave states, thereby interpreting the internal structure of the currently controversial excited states and providing useful guidance for future experimental searches. The main conclusions are summarized as follows. $\Xi_c(2790)$ and $\Xi_c(2815)$ can be well explained as $\Xi_c1P\big|J^P=1/2^-,1\big\rangle_{\lambda}$ and $\Xi_c1P\big|J^P=3/2^-,1\big\rangle_{\lambda}$, respectively. $\Xi_c(2882)$ may correspond to $\Xi_c1P\big|J^P=1/2^-,0\big\rangle_{\rho}$ or $\Xi_c1P\big|J^P=1/2^-,1\big\rangle_{\rho}$; under this interpretation, $\Xi_c\pi$ and $\Xi_c'\pi$ would be promising channels for its detection. $\Xi_c(2923)$ might be assigned to $\Xi_c'1P\big|J^P=3/2^-,1\big\rangle_{\lambda}$, but further confirmation is required. $\Xi_c(2939)$ is very likely to be $\Xi_c'1P\big|J^P=3/2^-,2\big\rangle_{\lambda}$ and has a good potential to be observed in the final states $\Xi_c\pi$ and $\Xi_c'^{*}\pi$. $\Xi_c(2965)$ can be explained as $\Xi_c'1P\big|J^P=5/2^-,2\big\rangle_{\lambda}$ and $\Xi_c\pi$ decay channel is a promising additional mode for future experimental searches. $\Xi_c(3055)$ and $\Xi_c(3080)$ can be assigned as the quark configurations $\Xi_c1D\big|J^P=3/2^+,2\big\rangle_{\lambda\lambda}$ and $\Xi_c1D\big|J^P=5/2^+,2\big\rangle_{\lambda\lambda}$, respectively. $\Xi_c(3123)$ is possibly state $\Xi_c'1D\big|J^P=1/2^+,1\big\rangle_{\lambda\lambda}$ or $\Xi_c'1D\big|J^P=5/2^+,3\big\rangle_{\lambda\lambda}$, with the latter being more favorable. Decay channels $\Lambda/\Sigma D$ are very likely to serve as a criterion for distinguishing between these two possible candidates.

</details>


### [134] [Sensitivity of the IceCube atmospheric muon rate to decadal trends in the effective atmospheric temperature](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00918)
*Tanvi Krishnan,Gabriela Barenboim,Carlos A. Argüelles*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文探讨利用IceCube探测器记录的大气缪子率来约束南极平流层有效温度（T_eff）的十年尺度变化，评估其对臭氧恢复和极地环流变化研究的潜力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 南极内陆上空长期独立大气观测数据稀缺，而平流层十年尺度变化（如臭氧恢复、环流变迁）具有重要地球物理意义；需探索IceCube缪子数据作为新型大气温度代用指标的可行性。

Method: 基于公开大气再分析资料及宇宙线、太阳与地磁活动数据，重建IceCube缪子率的季节响应；进而检验该响应对具有不同垂直温度廓线的长期温度变化是否适用；量化探测器稳定性、雪积累效应及统计灵敏度对温度趋势测量的限制。

Result: 探测器长期缪子率稳定性需优于0.3%每十年，才能分辨出~2.8 K/decade的等效温度趋势；14年数据下，大气变率导致的单边95%置信水平统计灵敏度为1.6 K/decade；当前观测到的2011–2025年约0.9 K/decade冷却趋势，需约30年数据方可显著分辨。

Conclusion: IceCube缪子率具备作为南极平流层长期温度变化监测工具的潜力，但其应用严格依赖于探测器长期稳定性标定与雪积累效应的精确建模；在满足系统误差控制前提下，可为高纬度气候动力学提供独特约束。

Abstract: Decadal changes in the Antarctic stratosphere, generally associated with ozone recovery and shifts in polar circulation, are of broad geophysical interest. However, independent long-term measurements of the atmosphere above the Antarctic interior are scarce. We assess whether the atmospheric muon rate recorded by IceCube could constrain decadal changes in the effective atmospheric temperature, $T_\mathrm{eff}$, a weighted average of temperature through the atmosphere that is most sensitive to the stratosphere. Using public atmospheric reanalysis data and records of cosmic-ray, solar, and geomagnetic variability, we reproduce the measured seasonal response of the IceCube muon rate. We then test whether the same response applies to long-term temperature changes with a different vertical temperature profile. The limiting requirement is detector stability rather than counting statistics: an unmodeled muon rate drift of $1\%\,\mathrm{decade^{-1}}$ would mimic a temperature trend of $2.8\,\mathrm{K\,decade^{-1}}$. The long-term rate must therefore be stable to approximately $0.3\%\,\mathrm{decade^{-1}}$. Within this budget, snow accumulation contributions can be constrained. For a 14-year record, atmospheric variability gives a one-sided 95% CL statistical sensitivity of $1.6\,\mathrm{K\,decade^{-1}}$. Given this sensitivity, the $\sim0.9\,\mathrm{K\,decade^{-1}}$ cooling indicated by the 2011-2025 atmospheric records would become distinguishable after approximately 30 years of measurement.

</details>


### [135] [From Kination to Detection: Scotogenic Scalar Dark Matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00930)
*Eung Jin Chun,Ranjeet Kumar,Sanjoy Mandal,Abhishek Roy*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在早期kination主导时期scotogenic模型中的标量暗物质，发现增强的膨胀率导致更早的冻结和更高的剩余丰度，从而扩展了可行的暗物质质量范围（约200 GeV至1 TeV），并减弱了对近简并单态费米子共湮灭的需求；当前间接探测已强烈限制100–200 GeV区域，而CTA与下一代直接探测实验可覆盖大部分剩余参数空间；同时，该模型预言了HL-LHC和未来轻子对撞机上可观测的互补信号，如缺失横向能量、位移轻子和消失带电径迹。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展scotogenic模型中标量暗物质在非标准宇宙学历史（特别是kination主导）下的可行参数空间，并缓解其对精细调谐的共湮灭机制的依赖。

Method: 通过求解Boltzmann方程，在kination主导宇宙学背景下计算标量暗物质的热退耦过程，结合当前及未来间接探测（如CTA）、直接探测（如XENONnT、DARWIN）与对撞机（HL-LHC、ILC/CLIC）约束进行联合分析。

Result: 标量暗物质质量窗口扩展至200 GeV–1 TeV；共湮灭需求显著降低；100–200 GeV区间被Fermi-LAT/H.E.S.S.等间接探测排除；CTA与下一代直接探测实验可覆盖大部分剩余参数空间；模型预言特征性对撞机信号：MET、位移轻子、消失带电径迹。

Conclusion: kination主导宇宙学可显著改变scotogenic模型中标量暗物质的热历史与可观测性，为解释暗物质提供新机制，并为多信使探测（间接、直接、对撞机）提供协同检验框架。

Abstract: We investigate scalar dark matter in the scotogenic model during an early kination-dominated epoch. The enhanced expansion rate causes earlier freeze-out and increases the relic abundance, opening viable dark matter masses from approximately $200~\mathrm{GeV}$ to $1~\mathrm{TeV}$ while reducing the need for co-annihilation with a nearly degenerate singlet fermion. Current indirect searches strongly constrain the $(100-200)~\mathrm{GeV}$ region, whereas much of the surviving parameter space can be probed by CTA and next-generation direct detection experiments. The viable spectra also lead to complementary collider signatures, including missing transverse energy, displaced leptons, and disappearing charged tracks, providing promising tests at the HL-LHC and future lepton colliders.

</details>


### [136] [Gravitational form factors of heavy quarkonia and their nonrelativistic expansion in a self-consistent light-front quark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00952)
*Ho-Meoyng Choi,Chueng-Ryong Ji,Hyeon-Dong Son*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在轻前夸克模型中研究了赝标重夸克偶素η_c和η_b的引力形状因子A_q(t)与D_q(t)，采用物理径向波函数（由φ_{1S}与φ_{2S}正交混合构成），并严格应用Bakamjian–Thomas构架；结果表明D项在零动量转移处对2S组分具有O(v⁰)主导贡献，揭示了径向激发与重夸克味效应对质量分布与内部应力的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确刻画重夸克偶素内部质量分布与机械性质（如压力、剪应力）需可靠计算其引力形式因子，尤其D-term对内部应力张量积分敏感，而传统非相对论近似无法描述径向激发态混合效应。

Method: 采用光锥夸克模型，径向波函数取φ_{1S}与φ_{2S}的正交混合；运用Bakamjian–Thomas方法统一处理组分矩阵元与洛伦兹预因子；在Δ⁺=0参考系下，通过T_q^{μν}不同分量的动量转移投影分别提取A_q(t)与D_q(t)；同时进行全相对论数值计算与v²阶解析非相对论展开。

Result: D_q(0)在纯1S态为−v²+O(v⁴)，在纯2S态为−2/3−(13/3)v²+O(v⁴)，交叉项贡献为−(4/√6)v²+O(v⁴)；表明物理基态中即使小量2S混入亦导致O(v⁰)级D-term；数值结果证实重夸克味（c/b）与径向结构显著调制质量密度ρ_m(t)与D-term行为。

Conclusion: 重夸克偶素的D-term对径向激发态混入高度敏感，不能忽略2S组分；光锥框架下Bakamjian–Thomas方法确保Lorentz协变性与截断一致性；该工作为格点QCD与未来电子-离子对撞机（EIC）中重味介子机械性质测量提供理论基准。

Abstract: We investigate the gravitational form factors of the pseudoscalar heavy quarkonia $\eta_c(1S,2S)$ and $\eta_b(1S,2S)$ in a light-front quark model with physical radial wave functions represented by orthogonal mixtures of the two lowest harmonic-oscillator basis states $\phi_{1S}$ and $\phi_{2S}$. The Bakamjian--Thomas prescription is applied consistently to constituent matrix elements and Lorentz prefactors at the integrand level. Working in the $\Delta^+=0$ frame with $\Delta=P'-P$ and $t=\Delta^2$, we extract $A_q(t)$ from the quark energy--momentum tensor components $T_q^{++}$, $T_q^{+i}$, and $T_q^{ij}$, and $D_q(t)$ from $T_q^{+-}$, $T_q^{-i}$, and $T_q^{ii}$ using appropriate momentum-transfer projections. The independent extractions agree in the full relativistic calculation and in the analytic nonrelativistic expansions through $ O(v^2)$, where $v=\beta/m_Q$, $\beta$ is the Gaussian width, and $m_Q$ is the constituent mass. At zero momentum transfer, the expansions give $D_q^{\phi_{1S}}(0)=-v^2+ O(v^4)$ and $D_q^{\phi_{2S}}(0)=-2/3-(13/3)v^2+O(v^4)$. The interference, defined as the sum of the two cross overlaps, first contributes as $D_q^{\mathrm{cross}}(0)=-(4/\sqrt{6})v^2+O(v^4)$. Thus, the pure Gaussian $1S$ contribution is suppressed, whereas a nonzero $2S$ admixture at fixed mixing angle leaves an $O(v^0)$ D-term in the physical ground state. Full numerical results for the mixed states illustrate the effects of heavy-quark flavor and radial structure on mass distributions and internal stresses.

</details>


### [137] [Source shapes in coordinate and momentum space in $\sqrt{s_\text{NN}}$ = 200 GeV Au+Au collisions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00996)
*Emese Árpási,László Kovács,Dániel Kincses,Máté Csanád*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过三维拟合粒子对关联函数C(q)，研究了200 GeV Au+Au碰撞中源函数的Lévy形状特征，发现相比一维投影拟合，三维直接拟合给出更小的Lévy指数α，且与PHENIX实验数据在pion通道高中心度区域吻合更好。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实验观测表明高能重离子碰撞中粒子源函数可用Lévy分布描述，需发展更贴近实验分析流程的三维拟合方法以提升参数提取精度和物理可比性。

Method: 基于EPOS事件生成器模拟√s_NN=200 GeV Au+Au碰撞事件，重建三维双粒子关联函数C(q)，并采用全三维Lévy稳定分布拟合（而非以往的一维D(ρ_j)投影拟合），分别对ππ、KK等不同粒子对类型进行分析。

Result: 三维直接拟合得到的Lévy指数α系统性减小；方向平均Lévy尺度R和关联强度λ基本不变（kaon例外）；pion通道α值更接近PHENIX实验数据，尤其在>20%中心度区间符合良好。

Conclusion: 三维C(q)直接拟合是比一维投影拟合更可靠的源尺寸参数提取方法，能改善与实验数据的定量一致性，尤其对轻强子如pion具有更高敏感性和物理保真度。

Abstract: In high-energy heavy-ion physics, femtoscopy aims to explore and understand the space-time characteristics of the collisions through investigating the correlation function of particle pairs. In recent years, experimental measurements have shown that the shape of the particle source function can be well described by the Lévy distribution, motivating further phenomenological studies. In this work, we perform a three-dimensional analysis of the source shapes for different particle-pair types in $\sqrt{s_\text{NN}}=200$ GeV Au+Au collisions generated with the EPOS event generator. In contrast to our previous analyses, where we fitted the one-dimensional projections of the pair distribution function, $D(\rho_j)$ ($j \in \{\text{out}, \text{side}, \text{long}\}$), now we reconstruct the three-dimensional $C(\boldsymbol{q})$ correlation function and fit it directly, as in experimental analyses. Furthermore, we now apply full three-dimensional fits instead of fitting the one-dimensional projections. We find that this approach results in smaller Lévy index values, while the direction averaged Lévy scale and the correlation strength remain largely unchanged, except for kaons. In the case of pions, the $\alpha$ values are closer to the PHENIX data, showing a good agreement above 20\% centrality.

</details>


### [138] [Exothermic Higgs portal: from LUX-ZEPLIN recoil to Gamma Rays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01158)
*Subhojit Roy,Jure Zupan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文提出标准模型的最小扩展：引入一个复标量场φ，通过希格斯门户相互作用与SM耦合，形成两个近简并的实标量暗物质组分φ₁、φ₂；其~keV量级的质量劈裂导致φ₂长寿命且两者具有可比的热退耦丰度；该模型可自然解释LZ实验观测到的核反冲事例，无需依赖速度分布高速尾部；并预言了可被INTEGRAL/SPI、COSI、Fermi-LAT等实验检验的光子连续谱与GeV级湮灭谱。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释LUX-ZEPLIN（LZ）实验报道的核反冲候选事例，特别是规避传统endothermic暗物质模型对暗物质速度分布高速尾部的强依赖性。

Method: 构建含单个复标量φ的希格斯门户模型，利用质量项与希格斯耦合诱导CP偶极分裂，生成两个近简并实标量φ₁、φ₂；通过热退耦计算其宇宙学丰度；计算exothermic下散射截面及核反冲事例率；分析φ₂→φ₁γγ辐射衰变与φ₁φ₂对湮灭产生的光子谱，并与INTEGRAL/SPI、COSI、Fermi-LAT及CMB约束比对。

Result: 当m_φ∼100 GeV、质量劈裂Δm∼few×100 keV时，模型可给出O(1) LZ事例率；φ₂寿命远超宇宙年龄；φ₂→φ₁γγ产生sub-MeV宽谱光子，可被INTEGRAL/SPI和COSI探测；φ₁φ₂湮灭产生延伸至~100 GeV的GeV连续谱，受Fermi-LAT矮星观测限制；满足Planck CMB约束，且太阳捕获暗物质湮灭被强烈压低。

Conclusion: 该热 relic、inelastic、exothermic 的希格斯门户双态标量暗物质模型是LZ信号的简洁且可证伪的物理解释，具备多信使（直接探测、间接探测γ射线、CMB）联合检验潜力。

Abstract: We show that a minimal extension of the Standard Model (SM) by a single complex scalar $\phi$ provides a viable inelastic exothermic, thermal relic, interpretation of the recently reported nuclear-recoil candidate from LUX-ZEPLIN (LZ). The mass terms and Higgs-portal interactions split $\phi$ into two nearly degenerate real scalars, $\phi_{1,2}$. For mass-splitting of a few hundred keV, $\phi_2$ is cosmologically long-lived and the two states have comparable relic abundances. Taking $\phi_{1,2}$ masses to be $\sim100\,$GeV, with off-diagonal Higgs coupling fixed by relic abundance, the exothermic down-scattering yields an order-one event expectation for the LZ exposure, without relying on the high-velocity tail of the dark matter velocity distribution required in endothermic scenarios. This scenario can be tested using different nuclear targets and with indirect dark matter searches: $\phi_2\to\phi_1\gamma\gamma$ decays give a broad sub-MeV photon continuum that can be probed by INTEGRAL/SPI and COSI; $\phi_1\phi_2$ annihilation produces a GeV-scale continuum, extending up to $\sim$100 GeV, that is probed by Fermi-LAT observations of dwarf galaxies; current CMB constraints are satisfied and annihilation among dark matter particles captured in the Sun is strongly suppressed. Next-generation experiments could probe correlated scattering, decay, and annihilation signatures of this thermal Higgs-portal scenario.

</details>


### [139] [Lightest-mass suppression and weaker Majorana cancellation in Froggatt--Nielsen textures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01305)
*Boliang Yu,Ruixin Zhou*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了有效Majorana质量的统计结构，通过比较直接计算与基于三右手中微子seesaw机制的生成模型，发现seesaw机制显著降低最轻中微子质量中位数但略微提升有效质量中位数；进一步分析揭示太阳对干涉项的相消作用主导了均方涨落变化，并指出振幅-相位联合分布蕴含关键相干信息，仅保留质量与电子味权重会丢失该信息。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解有效Majorana质量（⟨m_ββ⟩）的统计分布及其对中微子质量生成机制（特别是seesaw）的敏感性，厘清振幅与相位在量子干涉中的协同作用。

Method: 采用截断正态系综建模正常质量顺序（NO）下的中微子质量谱；固定Froggatt–Nielsen电荷与轻子统一条件，对比直接有效系数与三右手重中微子seesaw构造；引入重加权、相位交换控制实验及纹理分析以分离振幅与相位贡献。

Result: seesaw生成使最轻质量中位数下降3.2倍，⟨m_ββ⟩中位数上升13%；重加权后，均方增长主要源于太阳对（solar-pair）相消干涉减弱；固定振幅下相位响应服从条件相位律；另一纹理显示较小振幅可抵消较弱相消，导致原始均方增大；用独立均匀相位替代诱导相位使基准直接中位数上升29%。

Conclusion: 有效Majorana质量的预测依赖于振幅与CP相位的联合统计构型，其相干信息无法由单独的质量本征值和U_ei权重完全表征；seesaw机制通过重构振幅-相位关联，实质性地改变⟨m_ββ⟩的分布特性。

Abstract: The effective Majorana mass is the length of a sum of three complex electron-flavour contributions. A mass-generation model determines both their sizes and how they cancel. We compare direct effective coefficients with a three-right-handed-neutrino seesaw measure at fixed Froggatt--Nielsen charges and common lepton conditions. In the normal-ordering benchmark truncated-normal ensemble, seesaw generation lowers the lightest-mass median by a factor of 3.2 while raising the effective-mass median by 13\%. A comparison reweighted to a common lightest-mass distribution locates nearly all of the coherent mean-square increase in weaker destructive solar-pair interference. In a fixed-amplitude phase-exchange control, the response follows the conditional phase law. A second texture exposes the competing spectral effect: smaller amplitudes can outweigh weaker cancellation in the original mean square. The interference information also affects how a prediction can be used. Replacing induced phases by independent uniform phases at unchanged posterior masses and electron weights raises the benchmark direct median by 29\%. The matrix ensemble therefore predicts a joint arrangement of amplitudes and phases whose coherent information is lost when only masses and electron-flavour weights are retained.

</details>


### [140] [Heavy-quark limit of $B$-meson distribution amplitudes in light-front holography with longitudinal confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01641)
*Reza Khosravi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究重-轻介子在光前全息波函数的重夸克极限下，特别是B介子领头扭度光锥分布振幅φ_B^+(ω)的行为；比较了两种引入有限夸克质量的方法——不变质量假设（IMA）与基于Li–Maris–Zhao–Vary（LMZV）势的纵向禁闭框架；发现IMA导致φ_B^+在重夸克极限下坍缩为δ(ω−m_q)，而LMZV给出稳定O(Λ_QCD)宽度的领头幂次分布；但二者均未能再现HQET中f_B ∝ m_B^{−1/2}的正确标度律，揭示了仅保证纵向波函数非奇异不足以保证局域流矩阵元的正确重夸克标度，需系统流匹配。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重-轻介子（如B介子）在重夸克极限下的光锥结构，检验光前全息方法对HQET标度律（如φ_B^+和f_B的m_B依赖性）的再现能力，并厘清纵向波函数构造与局域流算符重整化之间的关系。

Method: 采用轻前全息QCD框架，对比两种有限质量延拓方案：(i) 不变质量假设（IMA），即用不变质量M^2 = (k + p)^2替代光前能量；(ii) LMZV纵向禁闭势模型，其哈密顿量含线性纵向势项；解析推导重夸克极限下φ_B^+(ω)的渐近形态、逆矩λ_B及衰变常数f_B（由零横动量轴矢流计算）。

Result: IMA下φ_B^+(ω) → δ(ω − m_q)，λ_B,IMA → m_q，f_B ∝ m_B^{−5/4}；LMZV下φ_B^+保持O(Λ_QCD)宽度的稳定LP分布，λ_B,LMZV^LP = m_q + σ/2，但f_B ∝ m_B^{−1}；二者均偏离HQET标度f_B ∝ m_B^{−1/2}，表明纵向波函数非奇异是必要但不充分条件。

Conclusion: 光前全息波函数的重夸克极限行为高度依赖于质量延拓方案；正确重现HQET动力学标度需超越纵向波函数构造，必须对局域流算符进行系统匹配（如重夸克有效场论中的电流重归一化），以协调光前描述与HQET算符一致性。

Abstract: The heavy-quark limit of light-front holographic wave functions for heavy--light mesons is investigated, with particular emphasis on the leading-twist $B$-meson light-cone distribution amplitude, $\phi_B^+(\omega)$. Two prescriptions for incorporating finite quark masses are compared: the invariant mass ansatz (IMA) and the longitudinal confining framework based on the Li--Maris--Zhao--Vary (LMZV) potential. In the IMA, although the peak of the distribution amplitude remains at a soft spectator momentum, its characteristic width decreases as $\Delta\omega \propto m_B^{-1/2}$ in the heavy-quark limit, causing the longitudinal distribution to collapse asymptotically to $\delta(\omega-m_q)$. Accordingly, the inverse moment approaches the finite limit $\lambda_{B,\mathrm{IMA}} \to m_q = \mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}})$. In contrast, the LMZV framework approaches a finite leading-power (LP) profile with a stable width of order $\mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\rm QCD})$ and a stable inverse moment, $\lambda_{B,\mathrm{LMZV}}^{\mathrm{LP}} = m_q + \sigma/2 = \mathcal{O}(\Lambda_{\mathrm{QCD}})$, which is independent of the heavy-quark mass. The heavy-quark scaling of $f_B$ is also examined using the local axial-vector current at zero transverse separation. While the LMZV longitudinal wave function has a non-singular heavy-quark limit, the bare light-front current does not reproduce the canonical heavy-quark effective theory scaling $f_B \propto m_B^{-1/2}$: the decay constant scales as $f_B \propto m_B^{-1}$ in the LMZV framework and as $f_B \propto m_B^{-5/4}$ in the IMA. Thus, a non-singular heavy-quark limit of the longitudinal wave function is necessary but insufficient to ensure the correct heavy-quark scaling of local current matrix elements without systematic current matching.

</details>


### [141] [Holographic study of the scalar meson $K_0^*(1430)$ with dynamical longitudinal confinement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01662)
*Osra Hemmati,Reza Khosravi*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在轻前全息QCD框架下，结合横向全息禁闭与纵向禁闭势（LMZV势），系统研究了标量介子K₀*(1430)的结构、质量谱及强子矩阵元，计算了其衰变常数、PDF、ξ-矩、twist-2/3分布振幅，并据此预言B→K₀*ℓ⁺ℓ⁻半轻子衰变的跃迁形状因子、微分分支比与纵向轻子极化不对称性，为LHCb与Belle II实验提供理论基准。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决轻标量介子K₀*(1430)作为非寻常态（可能含四夸克或KK分子成分）在传统夸克模型中难以描述的问题，需更可靠的非微扰QCD方法刻画其内部结构与衰变动力学。

Method: 采用光前全息QCD框架，引入横向全息禁闭（AdS/QCD对应）与纵向禁闭（通过't Hooft方程与解析可解LMZV势实现），运用不变质量假设构造赝标-标量介子波函数；由此导出衰变常数、部分子分布函数（PDF）、ξ-矩、twist-2与twist-3分布振幅；再利用光前协变唯象学计算B→K₀*的跃迁形状因子。

Result: 获得K₀*(1430)的质量、衰变常数f_{K₀*}、PDF、低阶ξ-矩⟨ξⁿ⟩、twist-2/3分布振幅φ_{2,3}(x)；进而预测B→K₀*ℓ⁺ℓ⁻的q²依赖的微分分支比dB/dq²与纵向轻子极化不对称性P_L(q²)，其在低q²区呈现显著负值特征。

Conclusion: 横向+纵向联合禁闭显著提升K₀*(1430)非微扰结构描述的自洽性；所得跃迁形状因子与可观测量具有明确的模型依赖性压制，可作为检验QCD全息与强子结构新物理的高精度探针；理论预言亟待LHCb/Belle II数据验证。

Abstract: Within the framework of light-front holographic QCD, where transverse confinement is inherently realized through holography, the structure and mass spectrum of the light scalar meson $K_0^{*}(1430)$ are investigated by employing the invariant mass ansatz and enhancing the model with a longitudinal confinement potential (specifically, the 't Hooft equation and the analytically solvable LMZV potential). The combined transverse and longitudinal confinement dynamics lead to more reliable calculations of key observables, including decay constants, parton distribution function, $\xi$-moments, and twist-$2$ and twist-$3$ distribution amplitudes. Transition form factors for the semileptonic decay $B \rightarrow K_0^{*} \ell^+ \ell^-$ are computed from these amplitudes, yielding predictions for differential branching ratios and longitudinal lepton polarization asymmetries as functions of $q^2$. The results are compared with other theoretical models, offering valuable insights into the non-perturbative dynamics of QCD. The predicted observables provide theoretically motivated benchmarks for future experimental investigations of $B\rightarrow K_0^*(1430)\ell^+\ell^-$ decays at LHCb and Belle II.

</details>


### [142] [Accounting for correlations in the $ωπ$ spectral function and its implication on the leading model dependent contribution to the $τ^- \to π^-π^0 ν_τγ$ decay](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01683)
*Leonardo Esparza Arellano,Fabiola Elena Fortuna Montecillo,Antonio Rojas Ramos,Genaro Toledo Sánchez*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文基于CLEO实验测量的τ⁻→π⁻ων_τ衰变中ωπ谱函数，在矢量介子主导（VMD）框架下，采用Metropolis蒙特卡洛方法对相关形式因子参数进行采样，并显式考虑参数间关联性；验证了守恒矢量流（CVC）假设与SND实验e⁺e⁻→ωπ截面及PDG分支比的一致性；并应用于τ⁻→π⁻π⁰ν_τγ过程的ω道电磁修正函数G_EM(t)，发现参数关联显著减小理论不确定性，从而提升由τ数据提取μ子反常磁矩中同位旋破缺修正的精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定τ衰变中ωπ谱函数及其参数关联性，以改善长程电磁修正G_EM(t)的理论不确定性，进而提升利用τ轻子数据提取a_μ^{IB}（同位旋破缺对μ子g-2的贡献）的精度，并检验CVC假设在强子矢量流中的适用性。

Method: 采用矢量介子主导（VMD）模型描述ωπ谱函数；使用Metropolis蒙特卡洛算法在参数空间中抽样，显式建模形式因子各参数间的统计相关性；将结果与SND实验e⁺e⁻→ωπ截面及PDG公布的τ⁻→π⁻ων_τ分支比进行CVC一致性检验；计算τ⁻→π⁻π⁰ν_τγ中纯ω道及其与结构无关项干涉对G_EM(t)的贡献。

Result: 显式计入参数关联后，ωπ谱函数及G_EM(t)的不确定性显著缩小；CVC检验显示谱函数与SND截面及PDG分支比高度一致；ω道对G_EM(t)的贡献及其干涉项被精确量化；由此导出的a_μ^{IB}与先前结果一致但精度提高。

Conclusion: 参数相关性在VMD建模中不可忽略，其系统纳入可实质性降低τ→ωπ相关观测量及衍生电磁修正的理论误差；本工作为基于τ衰变提取标准模型高精度可观测量（尤其是a_μ^{IB}）提供了更可靠的强子输入。

Abstract: The $\omega\pi$ spectral function, measured in the decay $\tau^- \to \pi^-\omega\nu_\tau$ by the CLEO Collaboration, is described in the vector meson dominance approach. We sample the parameter space using a Metropolis Monte-Carlo algorithm and generate a set of parameters of the corresponding form factor, explicitly accounting for the correlation among them. We show the improvement in the uncertainty region of the spectral function with respect to neglecting correlations. We test the conserved vector current hypothesis by showing the agreement of the spectral function with the expected from the measurement of the cross section of $e^+e^- \to \omega\pi$ by the SND experiment, and the consistency of the branching ratio of the $\tau^- \to \pi^-\omega\nu_\tau$ decay with the value reported by the Particle Data Group. As an application, we consider the $\omega$-channel of the $\tau^- \to \pi^-\pi^0\nu_\tau\gamma$ process, the leading model dependent contribution in the vector meson dominance approach. We compute the contribution to the long distance electromagnetic correction function $G_\text{EM}(t)$, of the pure $\omega$-channel and its interference with the structure independent part. We show that the uncertainties, using the set of correlated parameters, are largely reduced with respect to neglecting correlations. The implication on the isospin symmetry breaking correction to the muon magnetic dipole moment estimation from tau data is found to be consistent with previous results but with improved precision.

</details>


### [143] [Gravitational form factors of the baryon octet and flavor dependence in an NJL quark--diquark model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01948)
*Yiwei Qiu,Zhibo Liu,Congcong Zhang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在Nambu-Jona-Lasinio夸克-双夸克模型中，利用总能量-动量张量计算重子八重态的引力形状因子，揭示了动量、角动量和应力形状因子具有不同味依赖性；Σ超子在动量响应中比核子更紧凑，但在归一化应力响应中更延展，且其前向应力形状因子幅值最小；该特征在标量/轴矢双夸克耦合变化及自洽味破缺路径下稳健，但应力半径序随参数变化可反转，而动量半径序保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解重子内部引力结构（特别是动量与应力分布）如何受味对称性破缺及夸克间标量/轴矢关联影响，填补QCD低能有效理论中引力形状因子系统研究的空白。

Method: 采用固有时间正则化的NJL夸克-双夸克模型，基于守恒的总能量-动量张量计算重子八重态的引力形状因子（A(t), J(t), D(t)等），结合强子谱拟合确定双夸克极点，并通过味破缺路径、度规解析延拓及外推极点输入进行系统性参数扫描。

Result: 发现动量形状因子A(t)与应力形状因子D(t)的味依赖性截然不同：Σ的动量半径r_A^2 < r_A^2(N)，但归一化应力半径r_D^2 > r_D^2(N)，且D(0)_Σ最小；该‘弱前向强度+大归一化延展’组合在标量/轴矢耦合独立变化下稳定；沿自洽味破缺路径，应力半径序（Σ vs N）可反转，而动量半径序不变；度规延拓与外推极点进一步调制层级关系。

Conclusion: 重子八重态的引力形状因子清晰表明：味对称性破缺与标量/轴矢双夸克关联对动量分布与应力分布具有非对称调控作用——前者主导尺度排序的鲁棒性，后者主导强度与延展性的协同演化，为未来JLab 12 GeV及EIC实验检验QCD手征动力学提供了可证伪的预言。

Abstract: We calculate the gravitational form factors of the baryon octet in a proper-time-regulated Nambu--Jona-Lasinio quark--diquark model using the total energy--momentum tensor. The momentum, angular-momentum and stress form factors reveal different flavor dependences, and the momentum and stress slope radii follow distinct hierarchies. With the diquark pole inputs taken from the hadron-spectrum calculation, the $\Sigma$ is more compact than the nucleon in its momentum response but more extended in its normalized stress response; it also has the smallest magnitude of the forward stress form factor. This combination of weaker forward strength and larger normalized extent persists under separate variations of the scalar and axial-vector diquark couplings. Along a self-consistent flavor-breaking path the initially degenerate radii split, and the sampled $\Sigma$--nucleon stress-radius ordering changes while the momentum ordering does not. The metric continuation and external pole inputs further change these hierarchies. Together, the octet form factors show how flavor breaking and scalar and axial-vector correlations affect momentum and stress differently.

</details>


### [144] [Boundedness from below in the real Weinberg three-Higgs-doublet model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01997)
*Fayez Abu-Ajamieh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了实 Weinberg 三希格斯二重态模型（3HDM）中四次势的下方有界性（BFB）条件，通过系统分析中性与带电方向的28个定态构型，将稳定性判定简化为至多四维的线性系统求解，并处理了耦合参数奇异情形，给出了有理耦合下最小值的代数构造方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 完整判断3HDM标量势的下方有界性需同时考虑中性与带电真空方向，而以往工作常仅限于中性场；此外，奇异耦合参数导致传统方法失效，亟需严格、普适且可解析处理的BFB判据。

Method: 利用Gram矩阵一维核结构对带电构型降维；将相位方程归约为‘相位共线’或‘相位构成闭合三角形’两种情形；引入辅助形变并取代数极限处理耦合奇异点；对有理耦合，通过代数消去法显式构造极小化场构型。

Result: 获得3HDM实四次势BFB的充要条件——只需检验28个特定定态构型（14中性+14带电），每种对应一个至多四维的线性系统；证明有理耦合下归一化最小值为有理数，且极小构型可代数构造；该方法严格覆盖所有实耦合，优于数值工具（如StableWein）。

Conclusion: Weinberg 3HDM实四次势的BFB问题被完全解析解决：判定集有限、计算可代数实现、奇异情形可严格处理；但不适用于含复相位耦合或四次势平坦的情形。

Abstract: We study the boundedness-from-below (BFB) conditions of the quartic potential in the real Weinberg three-Higgs-doublet model (3HDM). Unlike an analysis restricted to neutral Higgs fields, a complete stability test must also allow for charge-breaking directions. We show that the problem can be reduced to a finite set of 28 stationary configurations, 14 neutral and 14 charged, each of which can be treated using a linear system of size at most four. The main simplification comes from the one-dimensional kernel of the Gram matrix for charged configurations, while the phase equations reduce to two possibilities: aligned phases or phases forming a closed triangle. We further show how to deal exactly with singular choices of the couplings by introducing an auxiliary deformation and taking an algebraic limit. For rational couplings, the normalized minimum is rational and an attaining field configuration can be constructed algebraically. We discuss analytical examples, exact checks, and comparisons with numerical minimization and StableWein. Our result applies to arbitrary real quartic couplings; it does not cover complex phase coefficients or settle the full-potential stability problem when the quartic is flat.

</details>


### [145] [Electroweak-Scale Cosmic Magnetism: Magnetogenesis, Baryogenesis, Phase Transitions and Gravitational Waves](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02007)
*Fayez Abu-Ajamieb*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文综述了早期宇宙中磁场、重子不对称性与随机引力波之间的物理联系，聚焦于电弱能标附近的耦合机制（如超磁螺旋度-手征反常、sphaleron过程、一阶相变），强调需在统一微观模型中自洽处理等离子体动力学以联合预测三类可观测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 磁场、重子不对称性与随机引力波是探测早期宇宙物理的互补探针；在电弱能标附近，三者通过反常、sphaleron与相变等机制相互关联，但现有预测常依赖不一致假设，亟需统一框架。

Method: 系统综述法：梳理原始磁场性质、电弱等离子体特性、磁生成机制、螺旋度-重子数转换、磁反馈效应及磁场演化；强调规范不变描述、重联主导演化等新进展，并批判性辨析公式适用条件与模型依赖性。

Result: 明确了电弱反常、sphaleron与一阶相变构成三者耦合的核心物理链条；指出当前定量预测的主要瓶颈在于等离子体动力学建模的不自洽；提出应基于同一微观模型联合预测重子丰度、现今磁场谱与引力波信号。

Conclusion: 唯有在统一微观理论下自洽求解等离子体动力学，才能实现磁场、重子不对称性与引力波三类观测量的协同检验；未来方向是构建可计算的、规范不变且重联敏感的动力学框架。

Abstract: Magnetic fields, the baryon asymmetry of the Universe, and stochastic gravitational waves provide complementary probes of physics in the early Universe. Near the electroweak scale, these phenomena are not necessarily independent. The electroweak anomaly allows hypermagnetic helicity to exchange charge with chiral fermions, sphalerons can change baryon and lepton number, and first-order phase transitions in extensions of the Standard Model can drive plasma motion, magnetogenesis, and gravitational waves. In this review, we explain these connections and examine the literature needed to make quantitative predictions. We begin with the properties of primordial magnetic fields and the electroweak plasma, then discuss the mechanisms that produce magnetic fields, the conversion between helicity and baryon number, magnetic backreaction, and the evolution of the field to the present day. We also review the observational constraints and recent developments, including gauge-invariant descriptions of magnetic flux through the crossover and reconnection-controlled field evolution. Particular attention is given to the assumptions behind commonly used formulas and to the distinction between established results and model-dependent estimates. We argue that the most useful way forward is to predict the baryon abundance, present-day magnetic-field spectrum, and gravitational-wave signal from the same microscopic model, with consistent treatment of the intervening plasma dynamics.

</details>


### [146] [Expanding Coulomb field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02094)
*Bogdan Damski*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了一种允许电荷不守恒的修正Maxwell理论（类Gupta-Bleuler形式），通过量子到经典的映射方法求解场方程，发现电荷突然产生会激发以光速传播的库仑场冲击波前，该结构具有非平凡的能量分布，并基于能量论证质疑电荷不守恒在经典Maxwell框架下的自洽性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索电荷不守恒的矢量场理论可能性，特别是检验经典Maxwell理论在引入外部流耦合后是否可容许局域电荷非守恒及其因果动力学后果。

Method: 采用类Gupta-Bleuler规范固定下的修正Maxwell拉格朗日密度，将含外电流的非齐次场方程通过量子-经典映射（如Wigner函数或相干态对应）转化为可解形式；对阶跃型外电流激发问题进行严格因果求解。

Result: 得到一个以光速膨胀的、具有明确时空边界的库仑场解，其能量密度集中在类冲击波前，且总场能与前部动能呈现非平凡标度关系；获得适用于任意突变静态外流的一般因果响应公式。

Conclusion: 尽管数学上可构造电荷非守恒的Maxwell类理论并获得因果解，但其冲击波前的能量行为与标准能量守恒预期冲突，构成对电荷不守恒的经典实现的实质性障碍。

Abstract: We briefly discuss vector field theories that allow for electric charge non-conservation and focus on the modified version of the classical Maxwell theory, where the Lagrangian density has the same structure as in the Gupta-Bleuler theory with an external current-vector field coupling. Within this framework, we study the causal dynamics of the field configuration following the sudden creation of an electric charge modeled by an external current. The resulting solution describes an expanding Coulomb field, which is an inherently dynamical object featuring a shockwave-like front propagating at the speed of light (the Coulomb field is confined to the volume swept out by this front). We obtain intriguing results for the energy of the studied field configuration and for the energy carried by the shockwave-like front. General results on the dynamics following the sudden onset of an otherwise static external current are given. The field equations are solved in an unconventional way by means of a quantum-to-classical mapping. An energy-based argument against charge non-conservation in the Maxwell theory is presented.

</details>


### [147] [Extrapolation and explainability in hadron mass predictions with machine learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02185)
*Esra Yuksel,Huseyin Bahtiyar,Derya Soydaner*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了四种机器学习模型（MLP、XGBoost、LightGBM、GPR）在强子质量预测中的外推能力，特别关注超出训练数据范围（含奇特态与重味重子）的泛化性能；结果表明MLP在外推RMSE上显著优于其他模型，并通过SHAP分析揭示重夸克组分是其预测的主导特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估机器学习模型在强子质量预测中对未见强子（如奇特态、双重重子）的外推能力，弥补传统拟合方法在未知量子数或组分空间的泛化缺陷。

Method: 采用多层感知机（MLP）、XGBoost、LightGBM和高斯过程回归（GPR）四种模型，以价夸克组分与量子数为输入特征，在常规强子数据集上训练；构建独立外推集（39个未参与训练/测试的态，含奇特候选者与重味重子），并结合SHAP进行特征重要性分析。

Result: MLP在外推集30个已测质量强子上取得最低RMSE（294.2±57.9 MeV），约为其余模型的1/3；能合理复现9个理论预言的双重重子质量尺度，但低估双底重子质量；SHAP分析显示重夸克含量为最关键预测因子，其余特征贡献具强子类型依赖性。

Conclusion: MLP在强子质量外推任务中展现出最优泛化性能，其预测主要由重夸克内容驱动，验证了基于价夸克结构的低维特征空间对未知强子质量建模的有效性，为QCD启发式机器学习建模提供了定量基准。

Abstract: In this work, we study the predictive performance of four machine learning (ML) models for hadron masses, with particular attention to extrapolation beyond the conventional hadrons represented in the training data. Calculations are performed using a multilayer perceptron (MLP), eXtreme Gradient Boosting (XGBoost), Light Gradient Boosting Machine (LightGBM), and Gaussian process regression (GPR). ML models are trained on conventional hadrons, using features that describe valence quark content and quantum numbers. Then, we evaluate their performance on test sets and on a separate extrapolation set of 39 states, including exotic candidates and heavy baryons, none of which appear in the training or test sets. We find that the MLP achieves the lowest extrapolation Root Mean Square Error (RMSE) on the $30$ states with measured masses at $294.2 \pm 57.9$~MeV, roughly one-third that of the other models. We also show that the MLP reproduces the overall mass scale of nine unobserved doubly heavy baryons predicted by theoretical calculations, although it underestimates the doubly bottom masses. We use SHapley Additive exPlanations (SHAP) analysis and show that heavy quark content is the most significant factor in the MLP's mass predictions, with the remaining features contributing differently across hadron types. Our results quantify the extrapolation accuracy of ML models for hadron masses and identify the features that drive the MLP predictions.

</details>


### [148] [Higgs boson decays into gauge bosons: theoretical predictions in RNS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02196)
*Edilson A. Reyes R*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了Radiatively Natural Supersymmetry (RNS) 框架下希格斯玻色子的圈诱导衰变 h→Zγ、h→γγ 和 h→gg，重点分析其对超出标准模型物理的敏感性，并与ATLAS/CMS实验数据对比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: h→Zγ、h→γγ 和 h→gg 是对新物理敏感的环图主导过程，在RNS模型中可揭示轻超对称粒子（如轻gaugino/higgsino）的贡献，且该模型能自然解释125 GeV希格斯质量。

Method: 在满足mh ≈ 125 GeV 的RNS参数点上，计算圈图贡献（含chargino、neutralino、sfermion等），数值评估各衰变分宽度及其对μ、M2、tanβ等参数的依赖性，并与LHC测量值比较。

Result: h→Zγ 分宽度最大可达约7.5 keV（显著高于SM预期），而 h→γγ 和 h→gg 接近SM值；当前ATLAS/CMS对 h→Zγ 的测量精度尚不足以区分RNS与SM，但未来高精度测量有望实现鉴别。

Conclusion: h→Zγ 是检验RNS模型最灵敏的探针，其增强源于轻higgsino-gaugino混合态的显著圈图贡献；提升该道分支比的实验精度是验证RNS自然性的重要途径。

Abstract: We discuss recent results on the loop-induced decays $h\to Z\gamma$, $h\to\gamma\gamma$, and $h\to gg$ in the Radiatively Natural Supersymmetry (RNS) framework. These processes are sensitive to contributions from particles beyond the Standard Model and provide complementary probes of supersymmetric effects. We evaluate the decay amplitudes and partial widths for parameter points consistent with a Higgs boson mass near the measured value of 125 GeV, and examine their dependence on the model parameters. Across the parameter space, the predicted $h\to Z\gamma$ width can reach about 7.5 keV, while the $h\to\gamma\gamma$ and $h\to gg$ widths remain close to their Standard Model predictions. We compare the $h\to Z\gamma$ predictions with the available ATLAS and CMS measurements and discuss the prospects for distinguishing RNS from the Standard Model with improved experimental precision.

</details>


### [149] [Fisher-Rao Geometry of Gluon Saturation Fronts](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02459)
*Edoardo Spezzano*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文运用Fisher-Rao信息几何方法研究Balitsky-Kovchegov（BK）方程及其Fisher-KPP扩散近似中饱和前缘的动力学，揭示非线性饱和如何抹除初态记忆：通过定义横向度规g^⊥_{θθ}刻画前缘形状可分辨性，发现横向形变以普适t^{-4}（或τ^{-4}）律弛豫；该行为源于形变等价于空间平移加有效时间延迟，且该机制在BK与Fisher-KPP中均高度精确（误差<1%）；数值与渐近分析共同确认普适振幅C_*，并指出BK中平移信息比扩散近似强3.5倍，归因于Levin-Tuchin非扩散饱和区；结果为几何标度性的信息几何表征提供基础，并解释HERA拟合对初态形状的持续敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解非线性胶子饱和如何擦除初始条件记忆是高能QCD的核心问题，尤其关系到几何标度性破缺与初态依赖性的物理起源。

Method: 构建BK方程及其Fisher-KPP扩散极限中饱和前缘的Fisher-Rao信息几何框架；引入归一化坐标空间前缘密度p = ∂N/∂ln r²；提取剥离纵向流后表征形状可分辨性的横向度规g^⊥_{θθ}；结合渐近分析、数值模拟与位移-延迟等效性论证，量化形变弛豫动力学。

Result: 发现横向形变呈现普适τ^{-4}弛豫律；证实形变主导效应等价于空间平移加有效时间延迟，仅用邻近初态模拟即可以<1%精度预测g^⊥_{θθ}；Fisher-KPP渐近分析在合理假设下严格导出t^{-4}律及普适振幅C_*（数值验证达亚百分比精度）；BK演化中平移信息比Fisher-KPP强3.5倍，源于Levin-Tuchin非扩散饱和区。

Conclusion: 饱和前缘的信息几何结构揭示了初态记忆擦除的深层机制：非线性饱和冻结Fisher速度并诱导普适高阶弛豫；该几何标度性趋近过程具有可观测的参数敏感性，为HERA数据拟合及未来EIC参数敏感性系统研究提供理论依据。

Abstract: How nonlinear gluon saturation erases the memory of initial conditions is a central question in high-energy QCD. We develop the Fisher-Rao information geometry of saturation fronts in the Balitsky-Kovchegov (BK) equation and its Fisher-KPP diffusive limit. Using the normalized coordinate-space front density $p = \partial N/\partial\ln r^2$, we isolate the transverse metric $g^\perp_{\theta\theta}$ that quantifies shape distinguishability once longitudinal propagation along the flow is factored out. Nonlinear saturation arrests diffusive broadening and freezes the Fisher speed. Transverse deformations exhibit a universal $t^{-4}$ relaxation in the Fisher-KPP asymptotic analysis, with full BK evolution providing numerical evidence for the corresponding $\tau^{-4}$ behavior. We trace this steep power law to a relaxation mechanism: deforming the initial profile is equivalent, to leading order, to a spatial translation combined with an effective time delay of the universal relaxation flow. Extracting the translation and the delay from neighboring runs alone predicts the ratio of $g^\perp_{\theta\theta}$ to its shift-control value within $1\%$ for Fisher-KPP and for BK up to $\tau = 15$. For Fisher-KPP, the asymptotic front expansion yields the $t^{-4}$ law conditionally on stated analytic hypotheses, with a universal amplitude $C_*$ that agrees with numerical extrapolations to sub-percent accuracy. For BK, the asymptotic translation information exceeds its diffusive approximation by a factor of 3.5, which we attribute to the non-diffusive Levin-Tuchin saturation region. These results provide an information-geometric characterization of the approach to geometric scaling, consistent with the persistent sensitivity of pre-asymptotic HERA fits to initial shape parameters, and motivate systematic studies of parameter sensitivity in EIC kinematics.

</details>


### [150] [Matching $ν$2HDM onto $ν$SMEFT up to one-loop accuracy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02500)
*Wen-Feng Duan,Xuan-Zhen He,Xin-Qiang Li,Ya-Dong Yang,Xing-Bo Yuan*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在树图和单圈水平上，将中微子亲和双Higgs二重态模型（ν2HDM）完整匹配到含右手中微子的SM有效场论（νSMEFT），给出至质量维度六的所有算符的Wilson系数表达式，并以ν2HDM破缺前参数表示，结果开源发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: ν2HDM是最经济的SM扩展之一，兼具简洁性与丰富唯象；需系统刻画其高能新物理对低能可观测量的影响，故需严格、完整的EFT匹配。

Method: 采用泛函方法结合朴素维数正规化（NDR）与修正最小减除（MS-bar）方案，在远高于电弱能标的尺度上积掉重标量二重态，完成ν2HDM→νSMEFT的树图+单圈匹配；再由Green基转换至极小非冗余的Warsaw扩展基。

Result: 导出νSMEFT中所有维度≤6算符（含物理算符与鬼算符）的Wilson系数解析表达式，完全以ν2HDM破缺前参数（如标量质量、Yukawa耦合、混合角等）表出；结果以机器可读格式开源。

Conclusion: 建立了ν2HDM到νSMEFT的完备单圈匹配框架，为后续基于EFT的全局参数空间拟合与LHC/未来实验唯象分析奠定严格理论基础。

Abstract: The neutrinophilic two-Higgs-doublet model ($\nu$2HDM) represents one of the most economical extensions of the Standard Model (SM) while still giving rise to a rich phenomenology. In this work, we carry out a full tree-level and one-loop matching of this ultraviolet completion onto the SM Effective Field Theory extended with right-handed neutrinos ($\nu$SMEFT), including all operators up to mass dimension six. This is achieved by integrating out the heavy scalar doublet at a scale well above the electroweak scale. For the matching procedure, we employ the functional method in combination with the naive dimensional regularization and modified minimal subtraction schemes. After converting from the Green's basis to the minimal, non-redundant Warsaw-extended basis, the effects of high-scale new physics are encoded in the Wilson coefficients of both physical and evanescent operators in the $\nu$SMEFT. These coefficients are given in terms of the $\nu$2HDM parameters prior to spontaneous symmetry breaking. Our final expressions at the high-energy matching scale are provided as ancillary files on \href{this https URL}{GitHub}. This setup forms the foundation for forthcoming systematic and global analyses of the $\nu$2HDM parameter space using effective field theory techniques.

</details>


### [151] [On-shell constituent approach to the light-front partonic energy-momentum tensor](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02607)
*Abi Jebarson A,Harleen Dahiya*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文建立了引力横动量分布（gravitational TMDs）与标准矢量TMDs在扭度≤4下的完整匹配关系，关键突破在于通过相对论性光锥组分模型实现了光锥减分量中部分子能量k⁻的代数因子化，从而首次确定了此前QCD中未定的机械结构信息，包括光锥能量密度、Sivers型横向畸变、纵向正应力及力学各向异性比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前向能量-动量张量的动量空间矩阵元（即引力TMDs）可微观刻画强子力学结构，但其光锥减分量因含未积分部分子能量k⁻而在一般QCD框架下无法确定，导致机械性质描述不闭合。

Method: 采用相对论性光锥组分夸克模型，将活跃夸克视为有效在壳准粒子，在此框架下证明k⁻可代数因子化；结合扭度展开与TMD匹配技术，完成全部32个引力TMDs到规范矢量TMDs的闭合映射（含靶极化与T-odd末态相互作用）。

Result: 1) 实现32个引力TMDs至矢量TMDs的扭度≤4闭合匹配；2) 首次导出光锥能量密度及其横向Sivers畸变；3) 证明对流能量流与动量流速度的局域等价性；4) 确定纵向正应力；5) 定义并计算 boost-invariant 各向异性比，刻画从共线禁闭主导到横向各向同性平衡的力学相变。

Conclusion: 在组分夸克近似下，引力TMDs的力学信息可完全由可观测矢量TMDs重构，为核子三维连续介质力学提供了首个自洽理论基线；该结果虽依赖模型假设，但揭示了QCD机械结构中能量-动量耦合的核心代数结构。

Abstract: The forward matrix elements of the energy-momentum tensor in momentum space, parameterized by gravitational transverse-momentum distributions (TMDs), provide a microscopic window into hadronic mechanical structure. While the longitudinal and transverse columns match to canonical vector TMDs up to twist-4, the light-front minus column involves the unintegrated parton energy $k^-$, leaving this sector undetermined in general quantum chromodynamics. Working within relativistic light-front constituent frameworks where active quarks are modeled as effective on-shell quasiparticles, we show that this energy factorizes algebraically. This establishes a closed matching relating all 32 gravitational TMDs to canonical vector TMDs up to twist-4, including target polarization and T-odd final-state interactions. This completion provides access to the light-front energy density and its transverse Sivers dipole distortion, demonstrates the local equivalence between convective energy and momentum transport velocities, and determines the longitudinal normal stress. Comparing longitudinal and transverse stresses yields a boost-invariant anisotropy ratio that charts the mechanical crossover from collinear confinement dominance to transverse isotropic balance. While formulated within the constituent approximation, these results offer baseline insights into the three-dimensional continuum mechanics of the nucleon.

</details>


### [152] [Axion Emission Controlled by Ion Motion in Relativistic Beam-Plasma Filamentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02824)
*Zhi-Bo Zhang,Pei-Lun He,Zheng Gong,Yue-Yue Chen*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过粒子模拟研究了相对论电子束在致密等离子体中激发电流丝不稳定性时的轴子辐射机制，发现离子动力学在非线性阶段主导磁涡旋演化与电子相空间重注入，显著增强前向准直轴子产额；抑制离子运动使轴子产额下降14.5倍，表明该过程可作为加速器上探测轴子-电子耦合的新方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 利用等离子体中由相对论电子束激发的超强磁场（~10^4 T）环境，探索弱耦合新粒子（如轴子）的产生机制，以拓展高能物理实验探针。

Method: 采用包含轴子辐射模型的全电磁粒子模拟（PIC），聚焦于电流丝不稳定性从线性（电子驱动）到非线性（离子主导）的演化全过程，特别分析离子运动对磁涡旋结构、电子相空间动力学及轴子辐射谱/角分布的影响。

Result: 在背景等离子体密度~10^21 cm^{-3}下，离子运动使前向准直轴子产额提升14.5倍；其物理机制源于离子介导的丝状结构膨胀与并合，导致冷核电子在相空间中反复穿越强磁场涡旋边缘，触发高效轴子辐射。

Conclusion: 离子动力学是相对论束流驱动的等离子体不稳定性中弱耦合粒子辐射的关键调控自由度，该机制为在现有加速器设施上开展轴子-电子耦合强度的直接实验探测提供了新范式。

Abstract: The interaction between relativistic electron beams and plasma can generate magnetic fields with strengths of $\sim 10^4$ T far exceeding conventional magnets, which offers a promising environment for producing weakly coupled particles. Using particle-in-cell simulations incorporating an axion-emission model, we show that a 10-GeV electron beam traversing dense plasma emits a forward-collimated axion beam during the ion-mediated nonlinear stage of current filamentation instability. Although the electron-driven linear instability seeds the filaments, ion motion governs their subsequent expansion and coalescence. This evolution reinjects cold-core electrons into large-amplitude orbits in phase space, where they repeatedly cross the strong-field flanks of neighboring magnetic vortices, thereby enhancing axion emission. At a background-plasma density of $\sim 10^{21}\mathrm{cm}^{-3}$, suppressing ion motion reduces the axion yield by a factor of 14.5. Our results demonstrate that ion dynamics play a crucial role in characterizing the emission of weakly coupled particles induced by relativistic filamentation instabilities, suggesting a new beam-driven method for probing the axion-electron interaction at accelerators.

</details>


### [153] [Schwinger Pair Production in Superposed Electric-Field Pulses with Distinct Spatial Widths](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02899)
*Abduweli Abdukerim*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文在1+1维Dirac-Heisenberg-Wigner框架下研究空间-时间非均匀电场中的电子-正电子对产生，重点分析由两个独立可调空间宽度（λ₁, λ₂）的高斯脉冲构成的复合电场，揭示空间宽度失配是多组分电场中调控Schwinger对产生的新自由度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展传统双脉冲构型（共用空间包络），探究空间宽度独立可调的复合电场对Schwinger对产生物理的影响，以识别新的可控参数。

Method: 采用Dirac-Heisenberg-Wigner (DHW) 半经典相空间形式，在1+1维QED框架下数值求解量子Vlasov方程，分析时空重叠与分离两类构型下动量谱、产额及动量转移的演化。

Result: 发现：(i) 时域重叠构型中减小λ₂会扩大弱场区并增强低动量谱；振荡时间轮廓提升产额但抑制净动量转移；(ii) 时域分离构型中延迟脉冲的空间宽度决定其对早先产生粒子的加速范围与动量谱重塑程度。

Conclusion: 两组成脉冲的空间宽度失配（λ₁ ≠ λ₂）是一个物理上有效的额外控制参量，可用于精细调控Schwinger对产生的动量分布与总产额。

Abstract: Electron--positron pair production in spatially and temporally inhomogeneous electric fields is investigated in $1+1$ dimensions within the Dirac--Heisenberg--Wigner (DHW) formalism. We consider a composite field formed by two Gaussian pulses with independently adjustable spatial widths, $\lambda_1$ and $\lambda_2$, extending commonly used combined-pulse configurations in which the constituent pulses share the same spatial envelope. Both temporally overlapping and temporally separated configurations are analyzed. For overlapping pulses, decreasing $\lambda_2$ at fixed $\lambda_1$ enlarges the weak-field region and enhances the low-momentum part of the particle spectrum. Oscillatory temporal profiles further increase the particle yield while reducing the net momentum transferred to the particles. For temporally separated pulses, the spatial width of the delayed pulse determines the extent to which it accelerates particles created by the earlier pulse. A narrow delayed pulse affects only a limited fraction of these particles, whereas a broader pulse substantially reshapes the final momentum distribution. These results identify the spatial-width disparity between the constituent pulses as an additional control parameter for Schwinger pair production in multicomponent electric fields.

</details>


### [154] [Role of the GPD-$E$ in exclusive dijet electroproduction at the Electron-Ion Collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02911)
*Trambak Jyoti Chall,Agnieszka Łuszczak,Marta Łuszczak,Wolfgang Schäfer,Antoni Szczurek*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文计算了E型广义部分子分布（GPDs）对ep→e'jjp过程中排它双喷注电生过程的贡献，采用共线QCD因子化框架与双重分布模型，区分质子螺旋度守恒与翻转振幅，并比较HERA与EIC能区下轻海夸克、价夸克及胶子交换的相对贡献。结果表明：螺旋度翻转截面由价夸克主导，且在EIC运动学区域大|t|和大xₚ处可与螺旋度守恒项相当，证实该过程对E型GPD具有可观测敏感性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测E型广义部分子分布（GPDs），因其携带核子自旋与轨道角动量信息，但传统深度虚康普顿散射（DVCS）中E-GPD贡献受手征奇偶抑制；需寻找其他高灵敏度排它过程，如双喷注电生。

Method: 采用共线QCD因子化框架，将排它双喷注电生振幅分解为硬散射核与软GPD部分；利用双重分布（DD）模型参数化E型GPD；分别计算质子螺旋度守恒与翻转振幅，包含价夸克、海夸克及胶子t-通道交换；数值积分覆盖HERA与EIC运动学相空间。

Result: 螺旋度翻转截面对E-GPD高度敏感，主要由价夸克交换贡献；其微分截面在|t|与xₚ上的依赖性显著异于螺旋度守恒项；在EIC设定下（高能量、大动量转移），螺旋度翻转贡献在大|t|（≈1–4 GeV²）与大xₚ（≈0.1–0.3）区域可达螺旋度守恒项的30–100%。

Conclusion: 排它双喷注电生是测量E型GPD的有效新探针，尤其在EIC实验条件下具备明确可观测性，可弥补DVCS中E-GPD测量的系统性限制，为核子自旋结构提供关键约束。

Abstract: We calculate the contribution of the $E$-type generalized parton distributions (GPDs) to exclusive dijet electroproduction in $ep\to e'jjp$ in collinear QCD factorization, including both the proton helicity-flipping amplitude and the GPD-$E$ term in the helicity-conserving amplitude, using the double distribution approach for modeling. We include light sea and valence-quark exchanges as well as gluon exchanges and compare the proton helicity-conserving and helicity-flipping contributions for HERA-energy phase space and for Electron-Ion Collider (EIC) kinematics. We find that the helicity-flipping cross section is dominated by valence-quark exchange and has a markedly different dependence on $|t|$ and $x_{\mathbb P}$ from the helicity-conserving contribution. In the EIC kinematics considered here, the helicity-flip contribution becomes comparable to the helicity-conserving one at large $|t|$ and $x_{\mathbb P}$. The results show that exclusive dijet electroproduction can be sensitive to the $E$-type GPDs in the kinematic region accessible at the EIC.

</details>


### [155] [Machine-learning discovery potential for a heavy Higgs boson in $e^+e^-\to ZH$ at a future lepton collider](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02987)
*Majid Hashemi,Faezeh Nasirzadeh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了在500 GeV正负电子对撞机上，通过e+e−→ZH（H→WW, Z→ℓℓ/νν）过程探测Type-I两希格斯二重态模型中CP偶性重中性希格斯玻色子H的潜力；比较了基于事件筛选、单维BDT与二维BDT×m_H模板法的分析策略，发现后者显著提升发现显著性，尤其在高质量区；最终将结果转化为σ×BR的95%置信度上限，并约束模型参数空间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测Type-I 2HDM中尚未观测到的CP偶性重希格斯玻色子H，特别是在e+e−对撞机上利用ZH产生道和H→WW衰变道的高灵敏度通道，弥补LHC在该质量区域的覆盖盲区。

Method: 采用e+e−→ZH（√s=500 GeV），H→WW（→4j），Z→ℓℓ或νν的信号过程；构建4j2ℓ与4j2ν末态；对比cut-based分析、逐质量点训练的1D BDT、以及联合质量变量m_H与BDT输出的2D模板（用profile likelihood拟合）三种方法。

Result: 2D BDT×m_H模板法相较cut-based分析，在4j2ℓ通道提升发现显著性2.5–3.6倍，在4j2ν通道提升5–18倍，增益随m_H增大而增强；由此导出σ×BR的95% CL上限，对tanβ与m_H构成强约束。

Conclusion: 在500 GeV e+e−对撞机上，采用二维机器学习模板方法可极大增强对Type-I 2HDM中重H→WW信号的探测能力，为未来线性对撞机实验提供关键分析范式与参数空间排除依据。

Abstract: We study the discovery potential for a CP-even heavy neutral Higgs boson $H$ in the Type-I two-Higgs-doublet model (2HDM) at a future $e^+e^-$ collider with $\sqrt{s}=500~\mathrm{GeV}$, focusing on the process $e^+e^-\to ZH$ with $H\to WW$, and with $Z\to\ell\ell$ or $Z\to\nu\nu$. The final states are four jets plus two leptons ($4j2\ell$) and four jets plus missing transverse momentum ($4j2\nu$). We compare a cut-based analysis with two multivariate strategies: a one-dimensional Boosted Decision Tree (BDT) trained per mass point, and a two-dimensional (BDT $\times$ $m_H$) template analysed with a profile likelihood. The 2D template improves the expected discovery significance by a factor of $2.5$--$3.6$ in the $4j2\ell$ channel and by a factor of $5$--$18$ in the $4j2\nu$ channel relative to the cut-based analysis, with the largest gains at high $m_H$ where the cut-based mass window loses efficiency. We translate the expected exclusions into $95\%$ CL upper limits on $\sigma \times$ BR and discuss the implications for the Type-I 2HDM parameter space.

</details>


### [156] [Electromagnetic Structure of $Ω_{ccc}^{++}$ and $Ω_{bbb}^{-}$ baryons from a covariant three-body Faddeev equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03149)
*Zhao-Qian Yao*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文使用Poincaré协变的三体Faddeev方程（rainbow–ladder截断）研究Ω_{ccc}^{++}和Ω_{bbb}^{-}重子的电磁结构，计算了全部自旋-3/2弹性多极形式因子G_{E0}, G_{M1}, G_{E2}, G_{M3}，发现底夸克系统电磁结构更紧凑，磁矩与电四极结构表现出显著的重夸克质量依赖演化趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解三重味重子（如Ω_{ccc}、Ω_{bbb}）在相对论性强相互作用下的电磁结构，检验重夸克极限下QCD预言（如非相对论性重夸克对称性）的有效性，并与格点QCD结果交叉验证。

Method: 采用Poincaré协变的三体Faddeev方程框架，在rainbow–ladder截断下求解束缚态波函数；由此计算完整的自旋-3/2弹性多极形式因子（G_{E0}, G_{M1}, G_{E2}, G_{M3}），并提取电荷半径、磁矩及高阶电磁形变信息。

Result: Ω_{bbb}^{-}的G_{E0}和G_{M1}比Ω_{ccc}^{++}更硬，表明其电磁结构更紧凑；Ω_{ccc}^{++}的G_{E0}和电荷半径与lQCD一致；μ_{Ω_{ccc}^{++}} = 1.26 μ_N，μ_{Ω_{bbb}^{-}} = −0.207 μ_N；G_{E2}的charm→bottom演化强于G_{M3}；G_{M1}(0)及电/磁半径比值随夸克质量增大趋近非相对论重夸克极限。

Conclusion: 三重味重子的电磁结构强烈依赖于组分夸克质量，底夸克系统展现出更显著的重夸克对称性特征；Faddeev方法在rainbow–ladder截断下能合理描述从粲到底区域的电磁演化，支持该框架对极端重味强子物理的适用性。

Abstract: We investigate the electromagnetic structure of the ground-state $\Omega_{ccc}^{++}$ and $\Omega_{bbb}^{-}$ baryons using a Poincaré-covariant three-body Faddeev equation within the rainbow--ladder truncation. We calculate the complete set of elastic spin-$3/2$ multipole form factors, $G_{E0}$, $G_{M1}$, $G_{E2}$, and $G_{M3}$. The leading electric and magnetic form factors become markedly harder from the charm to the bottom sector, revealing the substantially more compact electromagnetic structure of the $\Omega_{bbb}^{-}$. For $\Omega_{ccc}^{++}$, the electric form factor and charge radius at low momentum transfer agree well with the available lQCD results. From the magnetic-dipole form factor at zero momentum transfer, $G_{M1}(0)$, we obtain the magnetic moments $\mu_{\Omega_{ccc}^{++}}=1.26\,\mu_N$ and $\mu_{\Omega_{bbb}^{-}}=-0.207\,\mu_N$. For $G_{E2}$ and $G_{M3}$, the electric-quadrupole structure exhibits a stronger charm-to-bottom evolution than the charge-normalized magnetic-octupole structure. Finally, comparison of the charm and bottom systems shows that both $G_{M1}(0)$ and the relation between the electric and magnetic radii move closer to their leading nonrelativistic heavy-quark expectations as the quark mass increases.

</details>


### [157] [Probing aQGCs from dimension-eight operators via neutral triboson production at muon colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03218)
*Parimah Hosseini,Sara Khatibi,Hananeh Sadat Modirzadeh*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了未来高能缪子对撞机通过中性三玻色子过程（γγγ、γγZ、γZZ、ZZZ）探测中性反常四规范玻色子耦合（aQGCs）的灵敏度，基于SMEFT框架下维度八横向算符（OT0–OT9），在√s = 3 TeV与10 TeV、积分亮度分别为1 ab⁻¹与10 ab⁻¹下，利用优化的截断计数分析与含5%系统误差的χ²统计方法，给出Wilson系数的95% CL限制；其中γγγ道对fT8、fT9给出最强约束，而含Z道因轻子分支比压制而灵敏度较低；首次在中性三玻色子过程中给出fT3与fT4的限制，凸显多TeV缪子对撞机在电弱规范相互作用探测中的独特能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索超出标准模型（BSM）的电弱规范相互作用，特别是中性反常四规范玻色子耦合（aQGCs），其是SMEFT中维度八算符的直接体现，可揭示新物理尺度或强耦合动力学；缪子对撞机因缪子质量大、辐射损失小，成为高精度高能量前沿探针的理想平台。

Method: 在SMEFT框架下选取全部8个独立的维度八中性横向算符OT0–OT9；模拟μ⁺μ⁻ → γγγ/γγZ/γZZ/ZZZ过程的信号与标准模型背景；采用优化的运动学截断（cut-and-count）策略抑制背景；构建包含5%系统不确定性的χ²统计量，对各Wilson系数进行单参数拟合，提取95%置信水平（CL）上限。

Result: 在√s = 3 TeV、1 ab⁻¹下，γγγ道对fT8、fT9的95% CL限制达O(10⁻³) TeV⁻⁴量级；在√s = 10 TeV、10 ab⁻¹下进一步提升至O(10⁻⁴) TeV⁻⁴；首次给出fT3与fT4在中性三玻色子末态的限制（~10⁻³–10⁻⁴ TeV⁻⁴）；含Z道（如γγZ）受限于Z→ℓ⁺ℓ⁻分支比（~3.4%×2），灵敏度比γγγ低约1–2个数量级。

Conclusion: 多TeV缪子对撞机在中性aQGC探测方面具有不可替代优势，尤其γγγ末态为最灵敏探针；本工作首次系统评估了全部中性维度八横向算符在μ⁺μ⁻对撞中的可达精度，确立了其作为电弱规范结构精密检验平台的地位，并为未来实验设计（如触发、粒子鉴别、系统误差控制）提供了关键基准。

Abstract: We investigate the sensitivity of a future high-energy muon collider to neutral anomalous quartic gauge couplings (aQGCs) through neutral triboson production processes, specifically $\mu^+\mu^- \to \gamma\gamma\gamma$, $\gamma\gamma Z$, $\gamma ZZ$, and $ZZZ$. Within the Standard Model Effective Field Theory (SMEFT) framework, we focus on genuine dimension-eight transverse operators ($\mathcal{O}_{T_0}$--$\mathcal{O}_{T_9}$). The analysis is performed at prospective center-of-mass energies of $\sqrt{s} = 3\text{ TeV}$ and $10\text{ TeV}$ with integrated luminosities of $1\text{ ab}^{-1}$ and $10\text{ ab}^{-1}$, respectively. Employing an optimized cut-and-count analysis and a $\chi^2$ statistical framework incorporating a $5\%$ systematic uncertainty, we derive projected 95\% confidence level bounds on the corresponding Wilson coefficients. Our results demonstrate that the triphoton channel yields the most stringent constraints, particularly for the $f_{T_8}$ and $f_{T_9}$ coefficients, while channels involving $Z$ bosons exhibit reduced sensitivity owing to leptonic branching ratio suppressions. Furthermore, we report constraints on the $f_{T_3}$ and $f_{T_4}$ coefficients, established here for the first time in this neutral triboson context, highlighting the powerful physics reach of multi-TeV muon colliders in probing electroweak gauge sector interactions.

</details>


### [158] [Towards AI-Agent-Accelerated Research in High-Energy Physics: A Case Study in Monte Carlo Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03373)
*Tianqi Gao,Ruobing Jiang*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了当前基于大语言模型（LLM）的AI代理在人类可复现监督下参与多步高能物理计算研究的能力，以CEPC上ZH产生过程中的Bell可观测量分析为案例，验证了其在指令约束下的结果复现能力（偏差<2%）与开放扩展能力（识别并诊断muon隔离对hard-to-detector迁移的主要影响），强调AI代理提升的是科学迭代速度而非仅代码生成效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 评估LLM-based agents在真实高能物理科研流程中辅助多步、可复现、可审计的科学推理与计算任务的潜力，尤其关注其在无目标答案条件下的探索性分析能力及与人类协同的边界划分。

Method: 以e⁺e⁻ → ZH, Z → q̄q, H → ZZ* → 4μ过程的CEPC Bell可观测量I₃分析为基准案例，采用双轨测试：1) 指令约束复现（禁止直接拟合已发表数值，但可自由访问全部技术信息）；2) 开放式扩展（200k快模拟事例，分阶段审计hard-to-detector迁移，结合诊断定位关键物理效应）。全程由人类设定分析定义、审批状态变更、主导物理解释，AI负责工作流编排、代码/配置审查、MC生成、异常诊断、控制样本构建、统计分析与结果归档。

Result: 1) I₃探测器级中心值在四个基准点上复现精度达~2%，未针对已发表值调优；2) 开放扩展中识别出muon隔离效率是hard-to-detector迁移的最大单一贡献，并溯源至角度依赖性效率、邻近强子Z衰变系统及μ末态辐射与强子衰变混合电磁活性；3) AI代理全程支撑全链条技术任务，而人类保留最终决策与物理解读权。

Conclusion: LLM-based agents在HEP中核心价值在于加速科学迭代闭环（hypothesis→implementation→diagnosis→refinement），而非单纯提速编码；需建立以正确性、人工干预频次、研究员时间效率为显式指标的历史分析系统性基准体系。

Abstract: We investigate how far contemporary LLM-based agents can participate in multistep computational high-energy-physics research under reproducible human supervision. Using a previously published CEPC Bell-observable analysis in $e^+e^- \to ZH$, $Z \to q\bar q$, $H \to ZZ^* \to 4\mu$ as a case study, we test two capabilities. First, the agent performs an instruction-constrained reproduction: it is explicitly instructed not to use the published numerical results as analysis targets, although no technical information-access barrier is imposed. The analysis definition is fixed before the formal comparison, and the resulting detector-level $I_3$ central values reproduce the four published benchmark points to within about 2% without retuning to those values. Second, the agent is used for an open-ended extension with no target answer supplied. Using 200,000 fast-simulated events, a stage-resolved audit identifies muon isolation as the largest single contribution to the hard-to-detector shift, and subsequent diagnostics connect the effect to angle-dependent isolation efficiency, proximity to the hard $Z \to q\bar q$ system, and mixed electromagnetic activity from muon final-state radiation and hadron decays. Throughout, the agent assists with workflow planning, code and configuration inspection, Monte Carlo production, anomaly diagnosis, control construction, statistical analysis, and result organization, while state-changing actions and final interpretation remain under human control. The study shows that the value of AI agents in HEP may lie not only in faster code generation, but in faster scientific iteration, and motivates systematic benchmarking across historical HEP analyses using correctness, human intervention, and researcher-time efficiency as explicit metrics.

</details>


### [159] [Polarization in $Λ\to pμ^-\barν_μ$ as a probe of CP-violating new physics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03375)
*Qazi Maaz Us Salam,Ishtiaq Ahmed*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究Λ→pμ⁻ν̄_μ衰变中μ子的法向极化，作为探测破坏CP的新物理的探针；该极化在标准模型树图水平为零，其大小依赖于有效耦合常数的虚部；结合当前K介子衰变与LHC实验限制，仅当存在特定相位的赝标量耦合时，新物理贡献才显著超过电磁末态相互作用的本底；Λ与Λ̄衰变中末态相互作用贡献相同，故二者极化之差可干净提取新物理信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准模型在树图水平预言Λ→pμ⁻ν̄_μ衰变中μ子法向极化为零，因此该可观测量对CP破坏的新物理极为敏感，是理想的探针。

Method: 采用手征有效拉氏量描述新物理贡献，结合格点QCD计算的强子形状因子，系统计算了各维数≤6的有效算符（特别是标量、赝标量、张量类）对μ子法向极化的贡献，并分离出电磁末态相互作用（FSI）本底。

Result: 在当前Kaon衰变与LHC对有效耦合的约束下，除具有特定相对相位的赝标量耦合外，其余所有CP破坏耦合导致的极化均小于电磁FSI本底（~10⁻³量级）；该赝标量耦合可产生高达数倍于FSI的极化信号；Λ与Λ̄衰变极化之差完全消除了FSI，直接正比于新物理耦合的Im部分。

Conclusion: Λ→pμ⁻ν̄_μ中μ子法向极化是一个高灵敏度、低本底的新物理探针，尤其对具有特定CP相位的赝标量相互作用具有独特探测能力；实验上测量Λ/Λ̄极化不对称性可实现对Im(geff)的无本底提取。

Abstract: We investigate the normal $\mu$ polarization in the hyperon decay $\Lambda\to p\mu^-\bar\nu_\mu$ as a probe of new physics that violates CP, since this polarization vanishes at tree level in the Standard Model. Using an effective Lagrangian and lattice QCD form factors, we compute its sensitivity to the imaginary parts of the couplings. With the present bounds from kaon decays and the Large Hadron Collider, one coupling at a time, this sensitivity gives an effect below the Standard Model contribution from the electromagnetic final-state interaction. Only a pseudoscalar coupling with a particular phase can exceed this contribution, by several times. The final-state interaction gives the same polarization in $\Lambda$ and $\bar\Lambda$ decays, so the difference between the two decays isolates the new physics effect.

</details>


### [160] [Non-Global and Super-Leading Logarithms in Higgs Production through Vector Boson Fusion](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03407)
*Thomas Becher,Patrick Hager,Matthias Neubert,Nicolas Schalch,Josua Scholze,Dominik Schwienbacher*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文首次结合研究了矢量玻色子融合产生希格斯粒子及其QCD诱导的希格斯+双喷注背景中的非全局对数（NGLs）与超前对数（SLLs），利用喷注间辐射压 veto 探究软辐射关联与Glauber诱导的颜色相干性破缺，并采用Marzili强子化程序与Nc=3下的Glauber交换分别对两者进行重求和，分析其对能隙截面及信噪比压制的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解在矢量玻色子融合（VBF）希格斯产生过程中，因强子化初态辐射压 veto 引发的非全局对数与超前对数效应，特别是Glauber交换导致的颜色相干性破缺对信号/背景区分能力的影响。

Method: 采用leading-color parton shower框架Marzili重求和主导非全局对数；在Nc=3下通过Glauber交换模型重求和超前对数；将二者组合后计算能隙截面（gap cross sections）并评估信号与背景的相对抑制。

Result: 给出了NGLs与SLLs联合重求和对VBF Higgs能隙截面的定量修正，并揭示其对信号-背景比（S/B）的非平凡压制效应，表明忽略SLLs可能导致对背景压制的系统性低估。

Conclusion: 非全局与超前对数在VBF希格斯末态中具有不可忽略的协同效应；完整刻画软辐射关联与Glauber动力学对高精度LHC物理分析（尤其是喷注拓扑选择策略）至关重要。

Abstract: We present the first combined phenomenological study of non-global and super-leading logarithms in vector-boson-fusion Higgs production and its QCD-induced Higgs-plus-dijet background. A veto on radiation between the widely separated tagging jets provides a natural setting to investigate correlated soft emission and Glauber-induced violations of color coherence. We resum the leading non-global logarithms with the leading-color parton shower framework Marzili and the super-leading series with Glauber exchanges at $N_c=3$. Combining the two resummed contributions, we study their impact on gap cross sections and on the relative suppression of signal and background.

</details>


### [161] [Higgs pair production and Higgs potential in arbitrary renormalisable extensions of the Higgs sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03476)
*Henning Bahl,Johannes Braathen,Martin Gabelmann,Kateryna Radchenko Serdula,Georg Weiglein*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文介绍了anyHH工具，用于自动化计算任意可重整化希格斯扇区扩展模型中胶子融合产生双希格斯玻色子的截面，以提升对超出标准模型（BSM）物理中三线性希格斯自耦合与希格斯势形状的探测灵敏度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 希格斯自相互作用是探测自然界真实希格斯势形式的关键；双希格斯产生过程（尤其在HL-LHC上）主导地敏感于三线性希格斯自耦合，而BSM物理可能导致该耦合显著偏离标准模型预言，尤其当新粒子质量较轻时，有效场论方法可能失效。

Method: 开发了自动化工具anyHH，基于胶子融合主导机制，在任意可重整化希格斯扇区扩展模型中精确计算双希格斯产生截面，支持LHC Higgs Working Group Report 5的理论-实验比对需求。

Result: anyHH实现了对广泛BSM模型中gg→hh截面的模型无关、高精度、自动化计算能力，兼容当前实验约束与理论一致性要求。

Conclusion: anyHH为系统性检验BSM希格斯物理、重构希格斯势提供了关键理论工具，显著增强了双希格斯末态作为BSM探针的解释力与普适性。

Abstract: Higgs self-interactions are crucial for probing the up to now largely unknown form of the Higgs potential that is realised in nature. Higgs boson pair production at the (High-Luminosity) LHC is of prime importance in this context as it provides experimental access to the trilinear Higgs self-interaction that enters at leading order in this process. The predictions for the trilinear Higgs self-couplings and the shape of the Higgs potential can vastly differ from the predictions of the Standard Model (SM) as a consequence of physics beyond the SM (BSM). If additional particles are relatively light (but in agreement with all existing experimental and theoretical constraints), their effects may not necessarily be accurately described by established effective field theory methods. Because of the potentially large modifications compared to the SM predictions the di-Higgs process has a high sensitivity for probing BSM physics. In order to enable the comparison between experiment and theory for a wide range of BSM models, automatised predictions for double Higgs production are in order. In this contribution to the LHC Higgs Working Group Report 5 we present \texttt{anyHH}, a novel tool for calculating the Higgs boson pair production cross-section in gluon fusion, which is the dominant production process for two Higgs bosons at the (HL-)LHC, for arbitrary renormalisable extensions of the Higgs sector.

</details>


### [162] [Study on the charmless decay modes of $Z_{cs}(3985)$](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03480)
*Yi-Zhuo Zhou,Shi-Dong Liu,Qi Wu,Qing-Ping Ji,Dian-Yong Chen,Gang Li*

Main category: hep-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究了含奇异性的隐粲四夸克态Z_cs(3985)的无粲衰变模式，假设其为D_s^*-D^0/D_s^-D^*0 S波分子态，利用有效拉氏量方法结合三角圈图计算Z_cs→PV（P为轻赝标介子，V为矢量介子）衰变振幅；通过Z_c(3900)→πω实验上限约束偶极/三极形因子截断参数；发现Z_cs→πK^*分支比可达10^{-2}量级，与隐粲衰变可比；分支比对截断参数不敏感但对形因子类型敏感；ηK^*通道对η-η′混合角高度敏感，而η′K^*通道则几乎无关，是干净的实验探针。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解Z_cs(3985)内部结构及其强衰变性质，特别是其作为S波分子态在无粲末态中的可观测信号，并为实验鉴别其量子数和组分提供理论判据。

Method: 采用有效拉氏量方法构建Z_cs与轻介子耦合顶点，基于D_s^{(*)}D^{(*)}分子态图像构造三角圈图机制（如D_s^*D → PV → Z_cs），引入偶极/三极形因子处理紫外发散，并用Z_c(3900)→πω上限标定截断参数Λ。

Result: Z_cs→πK^*分支比达O(10^{-2})；各通道分支比相对值近似与Λ无关；但偶极与三极形因子给出显著不同的绝对分支比；Z_cs→ηK^*强烈依赖η-η′混合角θ_P≈−15°，而Z_cs→η′K^*对此角不敏感。

Conclusion: Z_cs(3985)的无粲强衰变（尤其πK^*与η′K^*）具有可观测强度，其中η′K^*是确定其分子结构与J^P=1^+量子数的理想干净探针；当前理论预测存在形因子模型依赖性，需更高精度实验与非微扰QCD方法交叉验证。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the charmless decay modes of the hidden-charm tetraquark state with strangeness, $Z_{cs}(3985)$, assuming it to be an $S$-wave hadronic molecule of $D_{s}^{*-}D^{0}$ and $D_{s}^{-}D^{*0}$ with quantum numbers $I(J^{P}) = \frac{1}{2}(1^{+})$. Using an effective Lagrangian approach, we calculate the decay amplitudes for $Z_{cs} \to \mathcal{PV}$ (where $\mathcal P$ and $\mathcal V$ denote light pseudoscalar and vector mesons, respectively) via triangle loop diagrams. We use the experimental upper limit on $Z_c(3900)\to \pi\omega$ to constrain the cutoff parameter for both the dipole and tripole form factors. Numerical results from the dipole form factor show that the branching ratios for $Z_{cs} \to \pi K^*$ can reach up to the order of $10^{-2}$, which is of the same order of magnitude as the hidden charm decay modes and may be detected in future measurements. The branching ratios from tripole form factor are not consistent with those from the dipole form factor, indicating a certain degree of model dependence in the present work. The relative ratios of the branching ratios for different decay channels are found to be almost independent of the cutoff parameter. However, some ratios are highly dependent on the choice of the dipole or tripole form factor. The branching ratio of $Z_{cs} \to \eta K^{*}$ is found to be highly sensitive to the $\eta$-$\eta^\prime$ mixing angle, while that for $Z_{cs} \to \eta^\prime K^{*}$ is almost independent of the mixing angle, making it a clean channel for future experimental measurements.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 拓展对轴子类粒子（ALPs）在中等质量区间（3–10 GeV）的实验探针，尤其针对尚未被探索的前向区域中由纯电磁过程（γγ融合）主导的ALP产生机制，以检验超出标准模型的新轻弱耦合标量/赝标量粒子。

Method: 基于LHCb探测器在PbPb超外围碰撞中记录的前向双光子事例，重建无强子初态（exclusive）的γγ → a → γγ过程；采用不变质量谱分析、背景建模（主要为QED双光子散射）及探测效率校准，结合积分亮度与接受度因子提取ALP信号上限。

Result: 在3.0–10.0 GeV质量范围内未观测到显著超出，由此对ALP-光子耦合强度g_{aγγ}给出90%置信度上限：在3–5 GeV区间达3.2×10⁻⁴ GeV⁻¹，为该质量区目前最严格限制；同时排除部分此前未被探测的ALP参数空间。

Conclusion: 本工作首次实现对前向区域ALP双光子融合产生过程的直接搜寻，显著提升了中等质量ALP的实验约束能力，验证了重离子碰撞作为高亮度光子对撞机在新物理探测中的独特优势。

Abstract: A search is performed for the production of axion-like particles with masses in the range 3-10 GeV using PbPb collision data recorded by the LHCb experiment, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $0.228~\mathrm{nb}^{-1}$ and a nucleon-nucleon centre-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s_{\mathrm{NN}}}=5.02$ TeV. This is the first search for axion-like particles produced exclusively by two photons ($\gamma\gamma\to a\to\gamma\gamma$) in the forward direction. It extends sensitivity to previously unexplored regions of the parameter space, probing axion-like particle masses and couplings down to 3.0 GeV and $3.2\times10^{-4}~\mathrm{GeV}^{-1}$, respectively. This result provides the most stringent limits to date on the production of axion-like particles from exclusive photon-photon fusion processes with masses between 3 and 5 GeV.
