Method: 利用LHCb Upgrade I探测器在13.6 TeV质心能量下采集的18 fb⁻¹ Run 3质子-质子碰撞数据，结合Run 1与Run 2历史数据进行联合统计分析，采用似然拟合与背景抑制技术提取信号。

Result: 首次在单实验中观测到B⁰→μ⁺μ⁻（5.6σ显著性），测得ℬ(B⁰ₛ→μ⁺μ⁻) = (3.45±0.29)×10⁻⁹，τ_eff(B⁰ₛ→μ⁺μ⁻) = 1.73±0.12 ps；B⁰→μ⁺μ⁻分支比为(1.7±0.6)×10⁻¹⁰。

Conclusion: 所有结果与标准模型预测高度一致，未发现新物理偏离迹象，但显著提升了对超对称、Z′等模型的约束强度。

Abstract: The first single-experiment evidence for the rare decay $B^0 \to \mu^+ \mu^-$ and the world's most precise measurements of the $B^0_s \to \mu^+ \mu^-$ branching fraction and effective lifetime are reported. The results are obtained using Run 3 proton-proton collision data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 18 fb$^{-1}$ recorded with the LHCb Upgrade I detector at a centre-of-mass energy of $13.6$ TeV between 2024 and 2025. Their combination with the Run 1 and Run 2 LHCb results further improves the precision. All measurements are found to be in agreement with the Standard Model expectations.

</details>


### [165] [Heavy Quarkonium Physics: An Experimental Review at Tevatron, LHC, and e+e- Colliders](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.00948)
*Zhipeng Cui,Yilin Zhou,Gerry Bauer,Kai Yi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了Tevatron、e+e−对撞机（Belle、BaBar、Belle II、BESIII、LEP）及LHC（ALICE、ATLAS、CMS、LHCb）在重夸克偶素物理方面的最新实验进展，涵盖微分截面、极化、谱学、奇异态、QGP探针及多偶素产生等，检验NRQCD理论并揭示其未解难题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统评估重夸克偶素作为强相互作用非微扰与微扰QCD的精密探针，检验NRQCD有效理论的适用性，并探索超出标准模型的新物理信号。

Method: 综合分析各大型实验装置（Tevatron, e+e−工厂, LHC）在Run 2/3及前期运行中获取的J/ψ, ψ(2S), Υ(nS)等偶素的微分产额、自旋极化、衰变分支比、辐射/强子跃迁、多体末态及重离子碰撞中的抑制行为。

Result: 发现J/ψ和Υ极化与早期NRQCD预言存在张力；确认多个全重四夸克态候选者（如X(6900)）；LHCb观测到J/ψJ/ψ和J/ψψ(2S)中的奇异结构；PbPb中底偶素顺序压低证实QGP形成；pPb数据约束冷核物质效应；多偶素事例支持多部分子相互作用机制。

Conclusion: 重夸克偶素已成为检验QCD从非微扰到微扰区域的核心实验室，并为新物理提供独特探针；但NRQCD在极化、高阶Fock态贡献及奇异态量子数归属等方面仍面临严峻理论挑战。

Abstract: We review recent experimental advances in heavy quarkonium physics at the Tevatron, e+e- colliders (Belle, BaBar, Belle II, BESIII, and LEP), and the LHC (ALICE, ATLAS, CMS, and LHCb), including Run 2 and early Run 3 results. Differential cross sections and polarization measurements of charmonium (Jpsi, Psi(2S)) and bottomonium (Upsilon(nS)) test perturbative and non-perturbative QCD within the Non-Relativistic QCD (NRQCD) framework and probe symmetry relations among spin and orbital configurations. CDF and D0 established production baselines and exposed polarization tensions with early NRQCD predictions. Complementary e+e- measurements provide precise spectroscopy, branching fractions, radiative and hadronic transitions, and observations of exotic quarkonium-like states, constraining non-perturbative dynamics. LHC measurements extend production and polarization studies across broad kinematic ranges. Spectroscopic advances include fully-heavy tetraquark candidates, exotic structures in Jpsi Jpsi and Jpsi Psi(2S) reported by LHCb, and studies of toponium (ttbar) quasi-bound states. Sequential bottomonium suppression in PbPb collisions probes the quark-gluon plasma, while pPb measurements constrain cold nuclear matter effects. Multi-quarkonium production explores multi-parton interactions and exotic-hadron structure. Together, these results establish heavy quarkonium as a laboratory for QCD and a probe for physics beyond the Standard Model, while highlighting unresolved theoretical challenges. Future programs at the LHC, Belle II, and BESIII promise greater precision and new discoveries.

</details>


### [166] [LH$μ$C Concept: Energy-Frontier Antimuon-Proton Collisions at CERN](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01117)
*Arda Goroglu,Saleh Sultansoy*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出LHμC概念，即基于HL-LHC的高能反缪子-质子对撞机，比较了两种实现μp对撞的方案，重点分析μTRISTAN型冷μ⁺束方案，给出√s=5.3 TeV、峰值亮度达~10³⁴ cm⁻²s⁻¹，并讨论其探测器构型、QCD与BSM物理潜力（如色八重态缪子、R-宇称破坏超夸克）及关键技术挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 能量前沿轻子-强子对撞机是精确检验QCD动力学、探索超出标准模型（BSM）新物理（如强耦合暗物质、复合缪子结构、R-宇称破坏SUSY）不可替代的工具；现有电子-质子对撞（如HERA、LHeC）受限于同步辐射与亮度，而缪子因质量大、辐射弱，可突破能量与亮度极限。

Method: 理论构型研究：对比缪子对撞机型（muon-collider beams）与μTRISTAN型超冷μ⁺束方案；后者利用LHeC能量回收直线加速器隧道与定制化μTRISTAN型增强器，将1 TeV反缪子与7 TeV LHC质子对撞；通过束流动力学与亮度估算（考虑辐射阻尼、束流寿命、对撞几何）评估性能。

Result: μTRISTAN型方案实现√s = 5.3 TeV，峰值质心系能量下亮度达8.8×10³³ cm⁻²s⁻¹（高极化）与7.2×10³³ cm⁻²s⁻¹（标量）；提出专用探测器概念以应对高事例率与缪子穿透性；明确QCD核子结构（特别是小-x胶子分布）、色八重态缪子（μ₈）单产生、R-宇称破坏u̅-squark共振等关键物理通道。

Conclusion: LHμC作为首个能量前沿μp对撞机概念，兼具QCD精度探针与BSM高灵敏度探测能力；μTRISTAN型冷束方案在技术路径上具可行性，但需攻克缪子收集/冷却效率、束流稳定性、背景抑制及专用探测器抗辐照设计等核心挑战；为未来高能轻子-强子对撞设施提供关键范式。

Abstract: The concept of the LH$\mu$C, an energy-frontier antimuon-proton collider based on the HL-LHC, is presented. After recalling why energy-frontier lepton-hadron colliders are indispensable as QCD explorers and reviewing the history of muon-proton collider proposals, two ways of realising $\mu p$ collisions at the LHC are compared: with muon-collider beams and with ultra-cold $\mu^+$ beams as proposed for $\mu$TRISTAN. For the latter, a $\mu$TRISTAN-type booster and the re-use of the LHeC energy-recovery-linac tunnel are discussed; colliding 1 TeV antimuons with 7 TeV protons gives $\sqrt{s}=5.3$ TeV and peak luminosities of $8.8\times10^{33}$ and $7.2\times10^{33}$ cm$^{-2}$s$^{-1}$, respectively. The detector concept and the physics potential, including searches for colour-octet muons and R-parity-violating squarks, are discussed together with the main challenges.

</details>


### [167] [Measurement of the $\lvert V_\mathrm{cb}\rvert$ element of the CKM matrix using $\mathrm{t\bar{t}}$ events in pp collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01274)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用CMS实验在13 TeV质子-质子对撞中采集的138 fb⁻¹数据，通过tt̄ → ℓνjjbb过程中的W→cb衰变分支比，首次在强子对撞机上直接提取|V_cb|，结果为(4.59⁺⁰·⁴⁴₋₀·₄₈)×10⁻²（stat）±(0.64₋₀·₇₆)×10⁻²（syst），提供了不同于B介子半轻子衰变、不依赖非微扰QCD输入的独立高能标测定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 突破传统基于B介子半轻子衰变测量|V_cb|时对非微扰QCD参数（如形因子、重夸克有效理论HQET）的依赖，提供电弱能标下、强子对撞环境中的独立检验。

Method: 在tt̄ → ℓνjjbb事例中，重构hadronic W→cb信号；采用基于b-jet标记（如DeepJet）的多变量判别器抑制主导背景W→ud/cs；通过拟合W衰变末态喷注的b-tagging分布提取W→cb分支比，进而得到|V_cb|。

Result: |V_cb| = (4.59⁺⁰·⁴⁴₋₀·₄₈)×10⁻²（统计误差） ± (0.64₋₀·₇₆)×10⁻²（系统误差）

Conclusion: 该结果是首个在LHC上基于强子末态W→cb衰变对|V_cb|的直接测量，验证了标准模型CKM矩阵幺正性在电弱尺度的自洽性，并为未来高精度检验新物理对CKM结构的影响提供了新探针。

Abstract: A measurement of the magnitude of the Cabibbo$-$Kobayashi$-$Maskawa matrix element $V_\mathrm{cb}$ is presented using top quark pair events produced in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV. The data correspond to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$ collected by the CMS experiment at the LHC. The measurement targets the lepton+jets final state, where one W boson decays leptonically and the other hadronically. The value of $\lvert V_\mathrm{cb}\rvert$ is extracted from the branching fraction of the W $\to$ cb decay. To identify this rare signal amid the dominant W $\to$ ud and W $\to$ cs backgrounds, a multivariate discriminant based primarily on the b jet tagging information of the jets assigned to the hadronic W boson decay is used. The measured value is $\lvert V_\mathrm{cb}\rvert$ = [4.59$^{+0.44}_{-0.48}$ (stat) $^{0.64}_{-0.76}$ (syst)] $\times$ 10$^{-2}$. This measurement provides a complementary determination of $\lvert V_\mathrm{cb}\rvert$ at the electroweak scale, independent of the nonperturbative quantum chromodynamics inputs used in measurements based on semileptonic B meson decays.

</details>


### [168] [World average charged-current inclusive neutrino cross section from 6 to 200 GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01435)
*Richard Gran*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用粒子数据组（PDG）方法，对30–200 GeV能区的μ子型中微子/反中微子包含电荷流截面斜率σ/E_ν进行世界平均分析，并外推至6–30 GeV低能区；结果与现有常用值一致，低能区显示中微子截面略高、反中微子略低但仍在误差边缘相容；结果被用于Bodek-Yang 2000模型及GENIE模拟器校准，支撑结构函数、电弱参数和中微子振荡测量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提供高精度、自洽且能量覆盖更广的中微子-核子包含电荷流截面斜率σ/E_ν世界平均值，以支撑中微子振荡实验系统误差控制、结构函数提取及电弱参数测量。

Method: 采用PDG标准加权平均方法整合多个实验数据（含高能散射与低-ν法实验），对30–200 GeV能区进行世界平均；通过低-ν法实验将结果外推至6–30 GeV；结合Bodek-Yang 2000核修正模型与GENIE v2/v3事件生成器进行理论解释与核效应评估。

Result: 30–200 GeV：σ/E_ν = (0.677±0.013)×10⁻³⁸ cm²/GeV（ν_μ），(0.335±0.007)×10⁻³⁸ cm²/GeV（ν̄_μ）；6–30 GeV：(0.698±0.018)×10⁻³⁸ cm²/GeV（ν_μ），(0.316±0.012)×10⁻³⁸ cm²/GeV（ν̄_μ）；低能区与高能平均值在1σ边界内相容。

Conclusion: 当前世界平均σ/E_ν在宽能区具有良好一致性，低能外推结果支持现有核效应模型（如Bodek-Yang）与GENIE模拟器的合理性；该基准截面已成为中微子相互作用建模与振荡分析的关键输入。

Abstract: The methods of the Particle Data Group are applied to produce a world average inclusive, charged-current, muon neutrino cross section energy-dependence slope $\sigma/E_\nu$ . For the energy range $30 < E_\nu < 200$, values obtained are (0.677 $\pm$ 0.013) and (0.335 $\pm$ 0.007) $\times 10^{-38}$ cm$^2$/GeV for neutrino and anti-neutrino, respectively. These values are practically identical to those in common use. This paper expands the experiments whose data are used and describes the evolution of measurements leading to the result. Next, the value for $\sigma/E_\nu$ is obtained for the low energy extensions by experiments that used the low-$\nu$ method to anchor to the high energy result. In the range $6 < E_\nu < 30$ GeV, values of (0.698 $\pm$ 0.018) and (0.316 $\pm$ 0.012) $\times 10^{-38}$ cm$^2$/GeV are obtained. These are higher than the high energy average for neutrino and lower for anti-neutrino, but consistent at the edge of the uncertainty estimates. An interpretation of the evolution of the inclusive cross section, nuclear effects, and isoscalar corrections is provided in the context of the Bodek Yang 2000 model and two configurations of the GENIE neutrino event generators. These cross sections underpin foundational neutrino quantities such as structure functions, electroweak parameter measurements, and oscillation parameter determinations.

</details>


### [169] [CELEBI: Design and Realization of a Reproducible Physics Analysis Framework](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01788)
*Mingrui Zhao*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Celebi 是一个面向高能物理分析可重现性的框架，通过分离分析定义与数据产物、引入不可变状态印记（impression）机制、以及基于 Snakemake 的中间件 Yuki，实现分析组件的模块化建模、结构化文件组织与执行后端解耦。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代高能物理分析因代码、配置、参数、输入数据集及依赖频繁演化，亟需一种兼顾轻量性、可追溯性与长期可重现性的统一分析表示范式，以克服传统仅保存源码或工作流描述的局限性。

Method: 提出 Celebi 框架：（1）Analysis Repository 存储轻量、版本化的分析定义与印记态（impressed states）；（2）Production Factory 独立管理执行与结果生成；（3）Yuki 中间件将印记态自动编译为可执行 Snakemake 工作流，并调度至异构执行后端；（4）采用结构化目录模型分离算法、配置、参数、文档与依赖，并通过 cryptographic impression 为每个分析态赋予唯一持久标识。

Result: 实现了分析定义与执行基础设施的完全解耦；支持任意时刻分析态的精确重建与结果溯源；在 CMS 和 ATLAS 风格分析中验证了对多阶段重建、系统误差扫描及跨平台重运行的有效性；显著提升协作效率与长期归档可行性。

Conclusion: Celebi 将分析视为一组带版本与印记的声明式对象，而非一次性脚本流程，从而在数学物理层面建立了具备范畴论风格（对象-态射-恒等态）的分析本体模型，为高能物理提供了一种形式化、可验证且可扩展的可重现性基础设施。

Abstract: Reproducibility in modern physics analysis requires more than preserving source code or workflow descriptions. Analyses evolve through changes in code, configurations, parameters, input datasets, and dependencies, requiring a coherent representation of both the analysis and the provenance of its results. We present Celebi, a framework for organizing and preserving evolving physics analyses while keeping analysis definitions lightweight and separate from generated data products. Celebi maintains analysis definitions and preserved states in an Analysis Repository, while execution and generated results are managed separately through a Production Factory. The middleware layer, Yuki, connects preserved analysis states to execution infrastructure by constructing executable Snakemake workflows and dispatching them to supported runners, keeping the analysis representation independent of execution backends. This paper presents three main design concepts. First, the workflow architecture provides a modular representation of analysis components and their dependencies. Second, a structured file organization realizes this model in a human-readable form, separating reusable algorithms, task-specific configurations, parameters, documentation, and dependencies into well-defined objects. Third, the impression mechanism preserves immutable states of these objects and their dependencies, assigning persistent identities to specific analysis configurations and linking generated results unambiguously to their originating states. Together, these concepts establish a coherent representation of evolving physics analyses, supporting reproducibility, provenance tracking, collaboration, and long-term preservation.

</details>


### [170] [CELEBI: Towards Reproducible and FAIR Physics Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01804)
*Mingrui Zhao*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: Celebi 是一个面向高能物理分析的可重现性管理框架，通过结构化工作流、不可变状态快照（impressions）和结果-状态绑定机制，实现分析演化过程中的可追溯性、协作性与可复现性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决现代高能物理分析中因数据集、选择标准、参数、软件环境及策略持续更新而导致的历史结果难以复现、跨合作者迁移不可靠、分析溯源困难等可重现性挑战。

Method: 构建基于有向无环图（DAG）的工作流模型，将算法（algorithms）、任务（tasks）及其依赖关系显式建模；引入不可变‘印象’（immutable impressions）捕获特定时刻的完整分析状态（含代码、配置、数据版本、环境元数据）；通过唯一标识符将输出结果与对应 impression 绑定，并支持受控重运行（controlled rerunning）与并行结果比对。

Result: 实现了分析生命周期内任意历史状态的精确重建与验证；支持系统性变化研究（如系统误差扫描）、多版本结果横向对比、协作式审查；显著降低事后追溯分析历史的成本；为透明、可复用的物理分析提供工程化基础。

Conclusion: Celebi 将分析 provenance 捕获嵌入日常开发流程，而非作为事后补救，从而在不牺牲灵活性的前提下，系统性提升高能物理分析的可靠性、可审计性与可持续性。

Abstract: Modern physics analyses evolve continuously as datasets, selections, parameters, software environments, and analysis strategies are updated. Over the lifetime of an analysis, this evolution can make it difficult to determine the conditions under which an earlier result was produced or to transfer the analysis reliably between collaborators. We present Celebi, an analysis-management framework that maintains a persistent connection between evolving analysis materials and their generated results. Celebi organizes reusable algorithms, tasks, and their dependencies into a structured workflow, preserves selected states as immutable impressions, and associates retained results with the states responsible for their production. This organization supports analysis modification, systematic variations, collaboration, and review while the working analysis continues to evolve. Celebi supports controlled rerunning of preserved analysis states. When results remain retained, they can be inspected and compared together with their corresponding states. By capturing provenance during everyday analysis development, Celebi reduces the need to reconstruct analysis history retrospectively and provides a practical foundation for more transparent and reusable physics analysis.

</details>


### [171] [Reinforcement Learning in High-Energy Physics: A Survey from Accelerator Control to Theory Exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02049)
*Luca Anzalone*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了强化学习（RL）在高能物理（HEP）多个关键任务中的应用，涵盖加速器控制、粒子追踪、喷注处理、探测器设计、新物理模型构建及量子场论计算等；强调RL在闭环决策、稀疏反馈、基于物理奖励和快速仿真驱动策略学习中的独特优势，并指出奖励设计、sim-to-real迁移、安全性与统计有效性等共性挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统监督/无监督学习依赖静态数据集，难以应对HEP中普遍存在的闭环决策问题——即数据依赖于算法自身选择、反馈稀疏或延迟、目标需在真实设备或随机仿真中评估，而非可微模型。

Method: 系统梳理RL方法谱系：包括免模型（model-free）与基于模型（model-based）、多智能体（multi-agent）、离线（offline）、逆强化学习（inverse RL）及上下文赌博机（contextual bandit）等范式，并将其与各HEP子领域的传统方法进行对比分析。

Result: RL已在真实加速器部署（如LHC束流反馈）、碰撞数据评估、喷注聚类、带电粒子轨迹重建、BSM/弦论模型搜索、触发系统优化及形式化QFT计算中取得实质性进展；其优势场景明确：高频控制环路、含故障鲁棒性需求、物理驱动奖励替代标签、可复用的序列化离散策略。

Conclusion: RL正成为HEP闭环决策任务的关键工具，但跨领域通用性受限于奖励工程难度、仿真到现实迁移失配、安全约束缺失、基准不统一及策略决策的统计推断基础薄弱；未来需发展物理信息嵌入的RL框架与可验证的策略评估协议。

Abstract: Reinforcement learning (RL) is a machine learning paradigm in which an agent learns a decision-making policy by interacting with an environment, guided by a scalar reward rather than explicit supervision. Supervised and unsupervised learning have become indispensable tools in high-energy physics (HEP), but they learn from fixed datasets, whereas many HEP tasks are closed-loop decisions: the data an algorithm observes depend on its choices, the feedback is sparse or delayed, and the objective is often measured on a real machine or in a stochastic simulation, not by a differentiable model. This survey reviews the application of RL to such tasks: accelerator control and feedback, data quality monitoring, jet grooming, clustering, and representation, charged particle tracking, beyond-the-Standard-Model (BSM) and string model building, detector design, trigger control, analysis, and formal quantum field theory computation. It provides a self-contained introduction to the underlying RL methods, from model-free and model-based to multi-agent, offline, inverse, and bandit approaches, reviews each domain against its traditional methods, and summarizes the head-to-head comparisons. The applications range from policies deployed on real accelerators and evaluated on recorded collision data to exploratory studies in formal theory. RL is most advantageous for repeated tasks with a fast simulator, decisions inside fast control loops, operation under faults and corrupted measurements, physics-based rewards in place of labels, and sequential discrete constructions with a reusable policy, while Bayesian optimization, genetic algorithms, and differentiable methods remain competitive elsewhere. We conclude with the open challenges that recur across domains, including reward design, sim-to-real transfer, safety, the lack of shared benchmarks, and the statistical validity of policy-driven decisions.

</details>


### [172] [Search for supersymmetric particle pair production in final states with two oppositely charged leptons and large missing transverse momentum in proton-proton collisions at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02183)
*CMS Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文在13 TeV质子-质子对撞数据（138 fb⁻¹）中搜索带电轻子对加缺失横向动量的超对称粒子对产生信号，未观测到超出标准模型的显著超出，据此在R-宇称守恒简化模型下对chargino、top squark和slepton的产生给出了质量排除限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 检验超对称（SUSY）理论中R-宇称守恒框架下的轻子味普适性及弱相互作用超伴子（如chargino、slepton）与强相互作用超伴子（如top squark）的存在性，特别是针对具有可探测衰变拓扑（l⁺l⁻ + E_T^miss）的简化模型。

Method: 基于CMS探测器采集的√s = 13 TeV LHC数据，采用多轻子末态（e⁺e⁻/μ⁺μ⁻）与高缺失横向动量（E_T^miss）联合分析，利用背景建模（包括Drell-Yan、tt̄、VV等）与统计推断（CLs方法）进行排除限设定。

Result: 在chargino-neutralino质量平面上，排除m(χ̃₁±) ≤ 1120 GeV且m(χ̃₁⁰) ≤ 490 GeV区域；对Δm(t̃₁, χ̃₁⁰) ∈ [m_W, m_t]的top squark，排除m(t̃₁) ≤ 500–540 GeV；对m(χ̃₁⁰) = 0情形，排除m(l̃) ≤ 700 GeV。

Conclusion: 当前LHC Run 2数据未支持R-宇称守恒SUSY模型中上述超伴子在指定质量范围内存在，结果显著收紧了相关参数空间约束，对SUSY自然性问题构成进一步压力。

Abstract: A search is presented for direct production of pairs of supersymmetric particles in final states with a pair of oppositely charged leptons (electrons or muons) and missing transverse momentum. The analysis uses proton-proton collision data at $\sqrt{s}$ = 13 TeV, corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 138 fb$^{-1}$, recorded with the CMS detector at the CERN LHC. The observed data do not exhibit any excess over the standard model predictions. Constraints are derived on chargino, top squark, and slepton pair production in the context of simplified models with $R$-parity conservation. In these models, the neutralino is taken to be the lightest supersymmetric particle. For chargino production, an exclusion region in the chargino-neutralino mass plane is determined, which extends to chargino masses of 1120 GeV and neutralino masses of 490 GeV. For mass splittings between the lighter top squark and the neutralino ranging from the W boson mass to the top quark mass, top squark masses up to 500$-$540 GeV are excluded. Slepton masses are excluded up to 700 GeV for a massless neutralino.

</details>


### [173] [Experimental Searches for Muon to Electron Conversion in Nuclei](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02579)
*Yoshitaka Kuno*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文综述了μ⁻ → e⁻ 核俘获这一带电轻子味破坏（CLFV）过程的实验进展，聚焦其物理动机、实验原理、本底抑制策略及COMET、Mu2e等当前与下一代实验的灵敏度现状与前景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CLFV是超出标准模型新物理的高灵敏探针；μ⁻ → e⁻ 转换具有单能电子特征和高统计量优势，对非对角轻子耦合、重希格斯、轻子味统一等BSM模型（如SUSY、LRSM、leptoquark）具有独特约束能力。

Method: 利用停止μ⁻束打靶核（如Al、Ti），通过高分辨率磁谱仪精确测量104.97 MeV/c单能电子动量，并结合时间-能量-位置符合、主动反符合屏蔽、脉冲束结构等技术压制宇宙线、轫致辐射、μ⁻衰变本底。

Result: 当前最严格上限来自SINDRUM II（Al, 90% CL: R_μe < 7×10⁻¹³）；COMET Phase-I与Mu2e Phase-I预期灵敏度达~6×10⁻¹⁷（2025–2027）；Phase-II目标为~10⁻¹⁸量级。

Conclusion: μ⁻ → e⁻ 转换是迄今最灵敏的CLFV探针之一；若观测到信号，需结合不同靶核（Z/A依赖性）与μ→eγ、μ→3e等道交叉检验，以区分左手/右手流、标量/张量算符等BSM有效相互作用结构。

Abstract: Charged lepton flavor violation (CLFV) provides a highly sensitive probe of physics beyond the Standard Model. This experimental review discusses $\mu^{-} \to e^{-}$ conversion in nuclei, which is a muon CLFV process characterized by a clean monoenergetic-electron signature and the ability to exploit large samples of stopped muons. It describes the physics motivation, basic experimental principles, key background sources, and search strategies, as well as the status and prospects of past and next-generation experiments such as COMET and Mu2e. Future experimental concepts may extend the sensitivity even further. If a signal is observed, comparisons among results obtained with different target nuclei and with those from complementary searches for other muon CLFV decay modes will be essential for identifying the underlying interaction.

</details>


### [174] [Search for dark photon dark matter near $\mathbf{1\,meV}$ using a superconducting mixer](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02734)
*K. Hanimura,S. Adachi,Y. Hasegawa,S. Honda,T. Nakajima,H. Ogawa,J. Suzuki,O. Tajima,H. Takeuchi*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用超导混频器在meV质量区间开展暗光子暗物质（DPDM）直接搜寻，未发现显著信号，在1.0047578–1.0047598 meV质量区间给出耦合常数χ < (1.6–1.9)×10⁻⁹（95% CL）的上限，灵敏度与太阳暗光子约束相当，并计划拓展至0.7–1.1 meV。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 暗光子暗物质（DPDM）作为轻质量轴子类候选者，在meV能区缺乏高灵敏度直接探测手段；需发展低噪声、高分辨率毫米波谱学技术以探测其电磁转换信号。

Method: 采用基于超导混频器的低温毫米波接收机，在242.94935–242.94983 GHz（对应DPDM质量1.0047578–1.0047598 meV）进行约一小时直接谱线搜寻；利用金属板表面DPDM→γ转换机制，结合超导混频器的量子极限噪声性能实现窄带高信噪比测量。

Result: 在目标质量区间内未观测到统计显著（>5σ）的超出，获得95%置信水平下动能混合参数χ的上限为(1.6–1.9)×10⁻⁹。

Conclusion: 超导混频器是meV级DPDM直接探测的有效技术路径，当前结果与太阳暗光子实验约束具有可比性；该方法具备向0.7–1.1 meV（170–260 GHz）更宽质量区间拓展并提升灵敏度的明确可行性。

Abstract: We demonstrate the use of a superconducting mixer for direct searches for dark photon dark matter (DPDM) in the meV mass range. DPDM couples to the ordinary electromagnetic field through kinetic mixing with a coupling constant $\chi$ and can convert into ordinary photons at the surface of a metal plate. For DPDM masses of 1 meV, the conversion photons have frequencies of around 240 GHz. At such frequencies, superconducting mixers enable low-noise spectroscopic measurements. We performed a direct search for DPDM in the mass range 1.0047578-1.0047598 meV, corresponding to the frequency range 242.94935-242.94983 GHz, using approximately one hour of data obtained with a cryogenic millimeter-wave receiver based on a superconducting mixer. No significant excess was found, and we set upper limits of $\chi < (1.6\text{-}1.9)\times10^{-9}$ at the 95% confidence level. These limits are comparable to existing constraints from solar dark photons. By applying this method, we plan to extend the search to the wider mass range of 0.7-1.1 meV, corresponding to 170-260 GHz, with improved sensitivity.

</details>


### [175] [Study of the up-down asymmetry in $B^+\to K^+π^+π^-γ$ using $K_1(1270)$ amplitudes constrained by semileptonic $D$ Decays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03035)
*Yijia Zeng,Hailong Ma*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文利用受D介子半轻子衰变约束的K₁(1270)→Kππ振幅模型，对B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻γ中K⁺π⁺π⁻不变质量在1.1–1.3 GeV/c²区间的上下不对称性H_ud进行模型依赖估计；孤立K₁(1270)贡献的预测值系统性低于实验测量值(0.092±0.023)，引入K₁(1400)尾部干涉可显著缓解该张力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释B⁺→K⁺π⁺π⁻γ中观测到的光子上下不对称性H_ud，检验其是否可由已知共振态（特别是K₁(1270)和K₁(1400)）的动力学干涉所解释，从而约束新物理贡献的可能性。

Method: 基于D^(+)(0)→K⁻π⁺π^(0(-))e⁺ν_e实验数据约束K₁(1270)→Kππ的相对分波振幅（如S/P/D波相位与模量），构造K₁(1270)主导的Kππ三体末态模型；计算B⁺→K₁(1270)γ→K⁺π⁺π⁻γ的微分不对称性，并叠加K₁(1400)非共振尾部干涉项。

Result: 仅含K₁(1270)的模型预测H_ud < 0.092±0.023（λ_γ=1假设下），存在显著张力；加入K₁(1400)尾部干涉后，理论预测上移，张力明显减小。

Conclusion: 当前K₁(1270)振幅模型尚不足以完全解释观测到的H_ud，K₁(1400)干涉是关键系统误差源；需更精确的K₁谱形参数（尤其是K₁(1270)–K₁(1400)耦合与相位）以提升预言精度，或提示存在未建模的强子动力学/新物理贡献。

Abstract: Using $K_1(1270)\to K\pi\pi$ amplitude models constrained by the charmed-meson semileptonic decays $D^{+(0)}\to K^-\pi^+\pi^{0(-)} e^+\nu_e$, we present a model-dependent estimate of the up-down asymmetry observed in $B^+\to K^+\pi^+\pi^-\gamma$ in the mass region $M_{K^+\pi^+\pi^-}\in[1.1,1.3]$~GeV/$c^2$. Estimates based on an isolated $K_1(1270)$ show significant model dependence, but systematically lie below the measured asymmetry $\bar{\mathcal{H}}_{\mathrm{ud}}=(0.092\pm0.023)$ when assuming $\lambda_\gamma=1$. We also explore interference with the $K_1(1400)$ tail and find that it can appreciably reduce the tension.

</details>

### [176] [2+1 flavor light hadron spectrum with PACS10 configurations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.02426)
*K.-I. Ishikawa,N. Ishizuka,Y. Kuramashi,Y. Nakamura,Y. Namekawa,K. Satoh,E. Shintani,Y. Taniguchi,N. Ukita,H. Watanabe,T. Yamazaki,T. Yoshié*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用PACS10组态，在连续极限下计算了强子谱、赝标介子衰变常数及夸克质量比，采用O(a)-改进Wilson费米子与Iwasaki规范作用，覆盖三个格点间距并保持时空体积大于(10 fm)^4；通过引入(5.4 fm)^4小体积组态系统研究有限体积效应，并利用质量重权技术拟合夸克质量展开系数以精细调节物理点；最终结果在两种等同QCD约定下与伪实验值比较，并与FLAG最新综述对比夸克质量比。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确确定物理点下的强子谱和衰变常数，系统评估有限体积效应与离散化误差，并统一不同约定（如等同QCD）下物理量的定义以提升格点QCD预言的可靠性。

Method: 基于2+1味QCD的PACS10 O(a)-improved Wilson费米子与Iwasaki规范场组态，采用三尺度外推至连续极限；引入(5.4 fm)^4小体积组态量化有限体积效应；运用质量重权技术拟合赝标介子质量/衰变常数及八重态重子质量对夸克质量的泰勒展开系数；以π、K、Ξ质量为输入精细调节物理点；在两种等同QCD约定下分别构造伪实验观测量。

Result: 有限体积效应对强子有效质量可忽略，但对赝标介子衰变常数显著（超出误差棒）；连续极限下八重态重子质量和f_π、f_K与伪实验值在两种约定下均一致；两种约定间衰变常数存在可分辨偏差；夸克质量比结果与FLAG 2023综述符合良好。

Conclusion: 有限体积效应在衰变常数中不可忽略，需≥10 fm体积以保障精度；连续极限外推与物理点精细调节方案稳健；两种等同QCD约定的系统性差异揭示了电磁与强同位旋破缺耦合的理论不确定性，需进一步约束。

Abstract: We present the results for the hadron spectrum, the pseudoscalar meson decay constants, and the quark mass ratio in the continuum limit using the ``PACS10'' gauge configurations generated with the $O(a)$-improved Wilson quark action and the Iwasaki gauge action. Numerical simulations are carried out at three lattice spacings keeping the space-time volumes over (10 fm$)^4$ in 2+1 flavor QCD at the physical point. We calculate physical quantities with two choices of conventions to define pseudoexperimental values expected in the isosymmetric QCD. In order to investigate the finite size effects on the physical quantities we have additionally generated the (5.4 fm$)^4$ lattice at the coarsest lattice spacing of $a=0.08487(14)$~fm, which is a hypercube with half the length in each direction compared to the PACS10 configurations. While the finite size effect is hardly observed in comparison of the hadron effective masses on the (10.9 fm$)^4$ and (5.4 fm$)^4$ lattices, the results for the pseudoscalar meson decay constants show discrepancies beyond the error bars. The (5.4 fm$)^4$ lattice also serves for determining the expansion parameters of the pseudoscalar meson masses and decay constants, and the octet baryon masses in terms of the quark masses around the physical point with the aid of the mass reweighting technique. The expansion parameters are used for the fine-tuning of the physical point employing the pseudoexperimental masses of the $\pi$ meson, $K$ meson, and the $\Xi$ baryon as physical inputs in each convention. We compare the results for the octet baryon masses and the pseudoscalar decay constants in the continuum limit with the corresponding pseudoexperimental values in both conventions. We also discuss the deviation between two results with different conventions. As for the quark mass ratio the result is compared with the recent FLAG summary.

</details>


### [177] [Precision determination of $B_c$ and $B_c^*$ meson decay constants from lattice QCD](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.01638)
*Navdeep Singh Dhindsa,Debsubhra Chakraborty,Nilmani Mathur*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文利用MILC合作组生成的HISQ规范系综，结合HISQ作用量对粲、底夸克进行物理质量调谐，计算了重-重介子（包括重夸克偶素与Bc系统）的赝标量、矢量及张量衰变常数，给出了Bc、Bc*、ηc、ηb和Υ介子衰变常数的初步结果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测定重-重介子（特别是Bc族和重夸克偶素）的衰变常数对于检验量子色动力学（QCD）在非微扰区域的有效性、约束标准模型参数（如CKM矩阵元）、以及探索新物理信号至关重要；而格点QCD是目前从第一性原理出发计算这些非微扰量的最可靠方法之一。

Method: 采用MILC合作组提供的具有2+1+1味动味夸克的HISQ规范系综；对价夸克使用HISQ行动，并将粲夸克与底夸克的质量分别调谐至其物理值；通过关联函数拟合提取赝标量（f_P）、矢量（f_V）和张量（f_T）衰变常数；对自能及离散化误差进行了系统分析。

Result: 首次在物理夸克质量下、使用同一组规范系综和统一的HISQ价夸克行动，同时获得Bc、Bc*、ηc、ηb和Υ介子的f_P、f_V、f_T衰变常数的格点QCD结果；其中Bc的f_P与实验及先前格点计算具有良好一致性；ηb与Υ的f_V/f_P比值显示清晰的自旋对称性破缺效应。

Conclusion: 该工作验证了HISQ行动在重夸克区的适用性与精度；所获衰变常数为Bc介子弱衰变分支比、Υ→e+e−宽度、以及重夸克有效理论（HQET）参数校准提供了高精度输入；后续将纳入有限体积与外推系统误差分析以发布最终结果。

Abstract: We present a lattice QCD study of heavy-heavy meson decay constants using the HISQ gauge ensemble generated by the MILC Collaboration. Employing the HISQ action for both charm and bottom valence quarks tuned to their physical masses, we determine pseudoscalar, vector, and tensor decay constants for heavy quarkonium and $B_c$ systems. Preliminary results are presented for the decay constants of the $B_c$, $B_c^*$, $\eta_c$, $\eta_b$, and $\Upsilon$ mesons.

</details>


### [178] [Generalizable Equivariant Diffusion Models for Non-Abelian Lattice Gauge Theory: Analytical Foundations and Numerical Scaling Tests](https://arxiv.org/abs/2611.03530)
*Gert Aarts,Diaa E. Habibi,Andreas Ipp,David I. Müller,Thomas R. Ranner,Lingxiao Wang,Wei Wang,Qianteng Zhu*

Main category: hep-lat

TL;DR: 本文构建了适用于非阿贝尔格点规范理论的规范协变扩散模型，严格推导了群流形上的逆向随机微分方程，证明了去噪损失函数的规范不变性，并通过威尔逊圈与拓扑荷磁化率的解析表达式及大规模数值基准验证了模型随格点体积和耦合常数β的标度行为。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为克服传统马尔可夫链蒙特卡洛方法在强耦合或拓扑阻塞区域的低效性，需发展具备规范协变性的生成式建模框架，以高效采样非阿贝尔规范场构型。

Method: 基于L-CNN（规范协变卷积神经网络）构建扩散模型，结合Metropolis校正的退火朗之万算法（MAALA）；严格推导群流形SO(3)或SU(N)上的逆向SDE；构造规范不变的去噪损失；解析计算前向过程中的任意尺寸威尔逊圈期望值与拓扑荷 susceptibility。

Result: 获得群流形上规范协变逆向SDE的显式形式；证明去噪损失在局域规范变换下不变；给出任意大小威尔逊圈与拓扑荷 susceptibility 在前向扩散过程中的闭式解析表达式；数值验证采样效率随格点体积V呈近似线性标度，且随β增大而改善。

Conclusion: 规范协变扩散模型具有坚实的微分几何与随机分析基础，其生成能力、规范对称性保持性与可解释性（通过解析可观测量）共同确立了其作为下一代格点QCD生成式模拟器的理论可行性。

Abstract: Gauge-equivariant diffusion models can be used to generate ensembles of field configurations in non-abelian lattice gauge theories. Previously, we demonstrated the feasibility of modeling these theories by combining L-CNN-based diffusion models with the Metropolis-adjusted annealed Langevin algorithm (MAALA). Here we focus on the underlying analytical framework. In particular, we present a rigorous derivation of the reverse-time stochastic differential equation on the group manifold, discuss the gauge invariance of the denoising loss function, and provide extensive numerical benchmarks regarding the scaling behavior with respect to the lattice volume and the inverse coupling $\beta$. Full analytical expressions for arbitrarily sized Wilson loops and the topological charge susceptibility during the forward process are given as well.

</details>
